- **The last of the charge** (`Game.StoneTank`): `Ceilings` 30, 55, 97, 97 - the bunker's ceiling and its death's pour 97; `HeadShares` 20, 20, 38; with all three down, `TickFinal` adds a percent every `FinalStepTicks` 132 to the tank and to the ceiling (`FFinalGiven`), full about 12 s after the blast.
- **Tests** (with the run's changes): `TRamBunkerTests` - its last life blows it up and the hunt begins there; dying at the middle it hunts from there; the hunt goes on after the bunker, empty-masted, no shells, nothing to hit, a ram still; `TStoneTankTests` - the bunker's ceiling 97, full 10 to 15 s after its blast, a percent at a time; `TestTheHuntLobsOneAtATimeMoreOften` in place of the pairs, `TestOffAPadTheHuntsShellIsForTheRoad`, `TestARowOffTheTopReachesFromLower`; `TBunkerShellTests.TestLobbedRightItRollsRight`, `TestTheRoadMarkIsTheRoadWithNoDeckInTheWay`, `TestTheRoadMarkStepsAsideFromADeckOverIt`. And: the pass off the left edge after the warning, the whole hull past it; a hero on a pad lets it pass; one on the road in the pass is thrown to the right, once; the turn unseen and silent, about a second and a half, the eye's red at the edge only at its end, the drive in bow first; the well mirrored; the bow a step behind him, standing and running, no faster than 1.6; it holds the left half; the surge after the eye's warning, to past 400 and back within 200 ticks; a hero on a pad carried back over its hatch, never backed into; one on the road behind its stern backed into and thrown to the right; a ram forward and the stand. Gone: the shove to the left edge, the hatch a step behind him, the throw back at the right wall. `TPadDriftTests`: `TestTheGroupsComeRightAfterTheWave` in place of the rows forever, `TestTheRowsFlyInFromThreeSides` in place of the flight from beyond the right edge. The peek on the screen, the mirrored art and the smoke are the painter's: checked live.
- First guesses for the live run: the turn's length and the peek, the bow's step behind him, the middle as its line, how often it surges, how far and how long, the speed coming in, the denser groups after the wave, whether the wave is long enough for the pass, how the rows from the left and from above read.

## Stage 16a as built (3.0.99)
- **Point A** (`FPastBunker`, `Game.Moon`): set by the bunker's blast (the head before the tick that blows up), cleared by a new level and by the End key's first two steps. `RestartLevel` past it: health `PastBunkerHealth` 5, the hero on the road at `PastBunkerHeroX` 352 - the right is his.
- **One setup, two callers**: the screen's `ram` event calls `StartRam`, and past the bunker it goes to `ResumePastBunker` - after a death and after the End key's third step alike, both through `RestartLevel`.
- **`ResumePastBunker`**: `TRam.StartPastBunker` (entered, the mast empty, `FKilled` the bunker, chased, the wave in; placed at the pass's end, `-RamWidth - PassBeyond`, and `BeginTurn` - the turn, the eye's red at the left edge, the drive in bow first, the hunt); the screen's own pads dozed, as the sweeper's wave left them; `TFarPlane.RideToEmblem` - from the start, ticked at the road's cruise as a chase rides it till the emblem's spire (`Emblem.Piece`) stands over the middle of the screen, its clock with it, then standing; `TPadDrift.BeginAfterWave` - the groups dense at once; the belt started; `TStoneTank.BeginPastTheHeads` - running, every head down, the ceiling 97 and the last percents on their clock from its start; the charge raised to the ceiling (`THeroOrbs.Charge`); the rumble; the boss track if it does not play.
- **The End key** (`DebugJumpToWork`): `FDebugJumps` steps through `DebugJumpToTrialEnd` (as before), `DebugJumpToRam` (level 2, the finale cleared - `DebugLoadWorkLevel` - and the hero at screen 13's checkpoint with the Duplet (`weaponDuplet`'s pickup) and 10 health, as a death in the fight brings him back, its event armed: the whole entrance) and `DebugJumpPastBunker` (`DebugJumpToRam`, then point A and `RestartLevel`); a fourth press does nothing. The walk's constants (`WorkLevel`, `TrialScreen`, `RamScreen`, `DebugJumpSteps`) under `DEBUGKEYS`.
- **Tests**: `TStoneTankTests.TestPastTheHeadsItFillsFromTheBunkersCeiling`; `TFarPlaneReadTests.TestRidesToTheEmblemOverTheMiddle` (level 2's layout); `TRamBunkerTests.TestPastTheBunkerItTurnsOffTheLeftEdgeAndHunts`. The dozed pads, the hero's place and the music are the game's: checked live.

## Stage 16b as built (3.0.100)
- **The heartbeat** (`Game.Heartbeat`, `THeartbeat`): it counts the beats and knows no charge - the game hands it a tempo every tick (`Tick`), so the later steps of the henshin can drive it by their own clock. The first tick after a stop strikes at once; then the tempo is gathered tick by tick and a beat is struck once a minute's worth of ticks is in, so a change of tempo keeps the phase. `Struck` - the first thump this tick; `Pulse(alpha)` - both thumps, the second `DubTicks` 8 (0.24 s) later at 0.7, each fading over 7 ticks. `ChargeTempo`: 60 a minute at `HeartbeatCharge` 97, 90 at full, straight between.
- **When it beats** (`TMoonGame.TickHeartbeat`, after the gauge's tick): past the bunker (`FPastBunker`) with the charge at 97 or more; otherwise still. A restart and a new level stop it, so point A starts it again with a beat. Each beat plays `heartbeat.wav` and beats the monitor.
- **The sound** (`tools/sounds/heartbeat.py`, `heartbeat.wav`, 0.6 s - shorter than a beat at 90): two dull thumps falling from 70 to 45 Hz with two overtones, a soft attack, under a low-pass at 220 Hz; the second 0.24 s on, quieter and a little higher. `DubSeconds` there and `DubTicks` in the code are one number.
- **The ride** (`TGaugeRide`, `Hud.Energy`): `Lift` from the slot, `Dock` back at once. 66 ticks, eased in and out (smoothstep), from the slot to the middle of the level's title line (`BigMessageY` of `Hud.Messages`, now public, plus half a big glyph; the middle of the frame across), growing to three times its size on the same curve; it stays up. The whole gauge draws at the ride's centre and scale - the disc, the halo, the rim, the arc and its head, the number and its sign. The slot stays open and empty: `WingWidth` follows the charge alone. `Heart` is still the slot, where the stone's orbs fly. Lifted by the bunker's blast and by `ResumePastBunker` - point A rides out of the slot again; docked by `RestartLevel` and the End key's level reset.
- **The pulse on the gauge** (`Draw(alpha, pulse)`): the disc swells by 8% on a thump and its halo lights by 0.45.
- **The monitor** (`Hud.Vitals`): `Paced` - no pulse of its own; `Beat` - a beat on the trace. One complex a double beat, on the first thump: the two thumps are one contraction. The monitor ticks before the heartbeat does, so it shows the beat a tick late.
- **Tests**: `Tests.Game.Heartbeat` (new) - `THeartbeatTempoTests` 4 (60 at 97, 90 at full, straight between, held outside), `THeartbeatTests` 9 (the first tick strikes; once a second at 60; every 22 ticks at 90; a faster tempo keeps what was gathered; a stop strikes again at once; the pulse peaks on the beat; the second thump weaker; the pulse dies before the next beat; a still heart has no pulse). `TGaugeRideTests` 7 in `Tests.Hud.Energy` (docked in the slot; lifted, from the slot; up at the title three times as big after its ticks; halfway is half of the way and of the growth; up it stays; a dock at once; a lift after the ride starts over). The sound, the pictures and the monitor's trace: checked live.
- First guesses for the live run: the ride's length and the height of the title line, the size up there, the tempo, the swell and the halo of a beat, the sound's pitch and loudness, the gap between the thumps.

## Stage 16c-1 as built (3.0.101)
- **The scene's clock** (`Game.Eclipse`, `TEclipseScene`): started at the brake, ticked by the game, cleared by a restart, a new level and the End key's level reset (`TMoonGame.ClearEclipse`). It knows neither the Ram nor the gauge: it tells its cues (`Corona`, `Diamond`) and its levels (`Darkness`, `Corona`, `Covered`, `Diamond`, `Gauge`). `Spares` from the start to the clear.
- **When** (`TMoonGame.TickEclipse`, after the heartbeat): past the bunker, the hero alive, the charge at `FullEnergy`, and the Ram whole on the screen in its hunt (`TRam.CanBrake`) - a 100 that comes while it turns off the left edge waits till it is in, so its eye is there to burn in the dark.
- **The brake** (`BeginEclipse`): `TRam.BrakeToAStand` - the hunt goes to `htBraking`: down by `Brake` a tick, the lamps burning; at a stand `htHalted`, the hiss and a jolt of `HaltJolt` 0.3 (`TickBrake`), the lamps held, the eye breathing; then it stands. Halted, `Contact` and `ThrowsOverTheHull` give nothing. `FBelt.Stop` - the run-down of 11a: the far plane, the fangs and the pads come to rest over `BeltRampTicks`. A hero in the hull (`TRam.InTheHull`) is thrown out with no wound, the side as a touch would throw him (`ClearHeroOfHull`).
- **No wounds**: `HurtHero` returns while the scene spares him - one door for every wound.
- **The corona** at `CoronaAt` = `BeltRampTicks` (66), the world at rest: up to 1 in 10 ticks, settled to 0.55 over 25. The disc's own light (the halo, the rim's track, the arc, the number) goes as it flares (`Covered`); the corona's glow behind the dark body, its ring of 3 over the rim (`THudEnergy.Draw(alpha, pulse, eclipse)`, `TGaugeEclipse`).
- **The darkness**: from the corona on, `DarkTicks` 40 to `DarkDepth` 0.85, smoothstep; one fill of the whole frame (`TEclipsePainter.DrawDark`, a deep violet-black) right after `dlFront`: under it the far plane with its sun, the fangs, the objects, the hull with its lamps, the pads, the tiles and the monsters; over it the hero, his orbs, the bullets and the lights after them, then the HUD. The Ram's eye is drawn once more over it at the dark's depth (`TRamPainter.DrawEyeOverDark`): the eye under the dark and the one over it add up to its own light.
- **The diamond** at `DiamondAt` = `CoronaAt` + 30: one point on the rim (a soft star of 9 with a core of 3.5, white), on the side of the hero as he stands at that tick (`DiamondAngleToward`, the disc's middle to his); lit, dark, lit, dark, lit - 6 ticks on, 5 off - and lit from the third on, for the beam of 16c-2 to leave it.
- **The track** (`TSoundBank.PauseMusic`): at the corona it fades out over `EclipseFadeSeconds` (the dark's 40 ticks) by a share of the volume of its own (`FHush`, apart from the level the scenes set) and is paused there (`Mix_PauseMusic`), not stopped; `ResumeMusic` goes on from that place. A paused track still plays as far as `Plays` tells, so the clear resumes it at once before a restart asks. A new track or a stop takes the pause away. The heartbeat beats on at 90.
- Not in it yet (16c-2): the control taken, the beam, the disc gone, the lift. The hero can still walk and shoot, unwounded, under the dark.
- **Tests**: `Tests.Game.Eclipse` (new) - `TEclipseTests` 10 (stands till started; spares from the start to the clear; the corona once, as the road stands; it flares and settles lower; the dark comes in with it, never lightens, holds at its depth; the track fades over the dark's time; the diamond once, after the corona; three blinks and lit after; a clear takes it all; outside the scene the gauge is bare), `TDiamondAngleTests` 2 (straight below is down; on the hero's side). `TRamBrakeTests` 6 in `Tests.Game.Ram` (no brake while it turns off the screen; to a stand with one hiss and the jolt; the lamps lit at the stand; it stands with its eye lit wherever he goes; standing it neither touches nor throws; one in the hull set out away from its middle, one clear of it not). The pictures, the overlay and the pause of the track: checked live.
- First guesses for the live run: the jolt, the corona's flare and its settled level, its size and ring, the dark's depth and tint and how fast it comes, the diamond's size, its blinks and the wait before it, the fade of the track.

## Stage 16c-2 as built (3.0.102)
- **The order reworked** (`Game.Eclipse`, decided 2026-11-05): the corona, the diamond, the disc as the eclipsed sun (`TGaugeEclipse`, `AimDiamond`, `DiamondAngleToward`) are gone. The clock from the brake: `CatchAt` = `BeltRampTicks` (66), the world at rest; `DimAt` = `CatchAt` + 30, he is up; the dark over `DarkTicks` 40 to `DarkDepth` 0.85 as before; `BeamAt` = `DimAt` + `DarkTicks` (136), the dark in. Cues `Caught`, `Dim`, `Beam`; levels `Holds`, `Hoisted` (whole ticks since the catch, -1 before), `Darkness`, `Fade` (the dark's share, the gauge goes out with it), `Beam` (0 at the top of the screen to 1 on him, over `BeamFallTicks` 8, smoothstep) and `BeamFlash` (1 as it lands, gone over `BeamFlashTicks` 12). Till 16d the catch is the stand-in for the orbs: nothing comes, he is just lifted.
- **The gauge** (`THudEnergy.Draw(alpha, pulse, fade)`): all of it fades with `Fade`; the corona and the diamond are taken out of it - it is the 3.0.100 gauge again, going out with the light.
- **The track** pauses at `Dim` over the dark's time (`EclipseFadeSeconds`), as 16c-1 paused it at the corona.
- **The beam** (`TEclipsePainter.DrawBeam`): two vertical strips of triangles on the `gsBeam` band (a Gaussian across - the soft edges), additive, from the top of the screen down to its front, then running out over `BeamTail` 48 below it - down to the road he was lifted off: a glow `BeamGlowAcross` 150 wide in moonlight (200, 186, 255) at `BeamGlowLevel` 0.4, and a white core `BeamCoreAcross` 44 (the band's faint thirds leave it about his width) at `BeamCoreLevel` 0.9. The heartbeat (`THeartbeat.Pulse`) swells both by `BeamPulseGrowth` 0.12 and brightens the glow by `BeamPulseGlow` 0.25; the landing widens the core by `BeamFlashGrowth` 0.6 and brightens the glow by `BeamFlashGlow` 0.5; landed, a halo (`HaloAcross` 96, level 0.45) round his body. Its x is his middle, its foot his feet (`TMoonGame.BeamFoot`). Drawn after the dark and the Ram's eye, before the orbs and the hero.
- **The control taken** from the catch: the held keys are not polled (their flags live on - a key held through the scene acts once he is let go), `THero.Fire` gives nothing, a queued bonus is dropped. Given back on the wheel (18b); till then he hangs at the top of the lift.
- **The hold** (`THero.Hold`, `HoldAt`, `Release`, `Held`): caught where he is, in the air or not - the throw, the fling, the boost and the deck he dropped through are let go, the pose is the jump's (legs drawn up). Held, `Tick` turns the arm after the cursor and cools the gun (`TickGun`), nothing else: no deck, no fall, no walk; `DeckUnderFeet` is nil, so the pads neither carry nor sag under him; `Command`, `ShoveX`, `RideBelt` and the throws do nothing. `Release` settles him - standing on ground, falling in the air. `Kill` and `Revive` let go; `ClearEclipse` releases him, so a restart, a new level and the End key's level reset all do.
- **The lift** (`Game.Hoist`, new, geometry alone): `PlanHoist` from his left edge and feet line - kept `HoistEdgeClear` 48 off both edges of the screen (room for the rite's ring; the right door at 479 is out of reach), and off the hull's box (`LeftAt`, `RamTop`, `RamWidth`, `RamHeight`, while the Ram is present) by `HoistHullClear` 16 wherever the body over the whole lift would come near it: to the side away from its middle, or the other side when that one has no room. Across at his own height first (`HoistDrawPace` 3 units a tick on average, smoothstep), then up one cell over `HoistRiseTicks` 30; at the top from then on. The game plans the path at the catch (`TMoonGame.CatchHero`) and puts him on it every tick (`TickEclipse`). No physics while held, so no pit and no door.
- **Tests**: `Tests.Game.Eclipse` rewritten - `TEclipseTests` 13 (stands till started; spares from the start to the clear; caught once, as the road stands; held from the catch to the clear; the lift counts from the catch; the light goes once he is up; the dark comes in and holds; the gauge goes out with the light; the track fades over the dark's time; the beam starts once the dark is in; it falls onto him and stays; the landing flashes and dies; a clear takes it all away), `TDiamondAngleTests` gone with the diamond; `Tests.Game.Hoist` (new) - `THoistPlanTests` 8 (free - straight up a cell; in the air - a cell too; by the left edge drawn right; by the right edge drawn left; left of the hull drawn left; right of it drawn right; a hull by the edge sends him the other way; on a pad over the hull not drawn), `THoistPathTests` 3 (it starts where he was caught; across first at his height, clear before the lift; then up to the top and stays). The hold (`THero` needs its sprite sets), the beam's picture and the gauge's fade: checked live.
- First guesses for the live run: the wait between the catch and the dim, the dark's pace, how fast the beam falls, its widths, levels and tail, the beat's swell, the landing's flash, the halo, the lift's pace and height, the clearance from the edges and the hull.

## Stage 16d as built (3.0.103)
- **The rite of the eclipse** (`Orbs.Moonrite`, new, `TMoonRite`): a choreographer of its own beside the waves of `TOrbRite` - one orb at a time on an outside beat, the ring held till it is told to draw in. Black orbs (`MoonOrbTint`), none armed. Its rings are the pattern `eclipse` (`PatternWaves` x `PerWave` = 60 seats). `MoonRiteScore`: the first gap 6 ticks (about 0.18 s), each heartbeat leaves the gap four fifths of itself (`GapStep` 0.8), never under 1 (about 0.03 s); every third orb out of the far city (`DeepEvery` 3); no orb off a face nearer him than 112 units (`Nearest`); the drawing in 30 ticks. A first guess of the stand-in run: the first orb sits about 29 ticks after the catch, the calls take about 4.5 s, the rings are full about 5.6 s after the catch.
- **Where they come from**: the k-th call takes seat k x 23 of the 60 (`SpreadStride`, a step coprime with the seats, about 0.37 of them), so seats one after another lie far apart and on the two rings in turn. An orb off a face takes the free face whose turn about the hero is nearest its seat's. The faces are `HarvestAfar`'s (`Orbs.Harvest`, new): none nearer him than `Nearest`; the twelve sectors about him take turns, each giving one of its faces at random, near or far, so they come from all sides and from all over the screen; thin air past the nearest for what the matter is short of. 3.0.103 first took `HarvestAround`'s one wave - the nearest faces from all sides - and the rite read as gathered from close by (the live run). An orb off a face shows through in 5 ticks, the face marked, and flies at once - 10 ticks and one for every 12 units, 30 at most, a spiral about him (`SpiralTo`). An orb out of the depth is born a speck (0.15 of its size, 0.35 of its light) behind the hero at a point of the far city the dice pick and grows over a flight of 34 ticks, coming out from behind him on the way. Seated, `Gathered` counts it; it only grows.
- **The matter** (`TMoonGame.EclipseMatter`): the screen's cells and pads (`MatterAround`), the hull's box (`HullBox`, shared with the lift's plan), and on the chase's screen a column 8 units wide down the middle of every fang on the screen, from its top to the bottom edge - its tip gives the orbs, not the box of its picture. The depth (`TFarPlaneDef.CityPoints`): the middle of every building of the city whose middle is on the screen, where the far plane stands.
- **The pattern** (`Orbs.Patterns`, `EclipseSeat`, `EclipsePatternName`): two rings about the chest (2 units over the middle of the body), of radius 30 and 42, 30 seats each, face on, a little in front of him (depth 0.2). The places of the figure stand on the rings in turn - even ones inner, odd ones outer, every outer seat halfway between two inner ones; the three waves fill both together, each wave every third place. The inner ring turns once in 360 ticks, the outer one the other way; the light runs round them (`Lit`). The live run of the first build asked for more orbs: it had one ring of 30. A level file may name it for the ice form's rite too.
- **The ring beats**: the rite is handed the heart each tick (`TRiteBeat`: struck, pulse); every seat is drawn in toward the hero by 0.12 of the pulse (`RingDrawIn`) and its orb swells by 0.15 of it (`SeatSwell`). Ringed, it beats in place until `DrawIn`.
- **The drawing in**: every orb to a point of his body (`BodyEntry`), waiting and taking its share of the 30 ticks as the ice form's do (`DrawnInto`); at the end all are let go: `mvFinished`. A collapse (a death) implodes them and tells nothing.
- **Shared with the ice form's rite** (`Orbs.Rite`, interface): `EasedInOut`, `SpiralTo`, `DrawnInto`, `BodyEntry`; `TOrbRite.Fly`, `Converge` and `EntryIn` call them, the numbers unchanged.
- **The scene's clock** (`Game.Eclipse`, `TEclipseScene.Tick(TRingProgress)`): the game hands it how far the ring has come (`Gathered`, `Count`), as the rite left it a tick ago. Cues: `Caught` at `CatchAt` (66) as before; `Lifted` - the first tick with an orb seated, the catch done (`Hoisted` counts from it, -1 before); `Beam` - the ring full; `DrawIn` - `BeamFallTicks` + `RingInBeamTicks` (50) after the beam. `DimAt` and `BeamAt` are gone.
- **The dark is the rite's**: it follows `DarkDepth` x the ring's share, never lighter and never quicker than `DarkDepth` over `DarkTicks` (40), and goes all the way once the ring is full. `Darkness(alpha)` lies between the last tick and this one; `Fade` is its share, the gauge going out with it. The track fades from `Lifted` over `RiteFadeSeconds` 3.5.
- **The ripples** (`TEclipsePainter.DrawRipple`): on every beat a ring of faint moonlit points runs from 48 to 170 units out from his middle over 22 ticks and fades, as strong as the dark is (`Ripple`), from the lift till the orbs go in. Drawn after the Ram's eye over the dark, before the beam. `THeartbeat.SinceBeat` tells the ticks since the beat.
- **The heart** (`Game.Heartbeat.RiteTempo`): while the scene runs, 90 a minute with the ring empty (where `ChargeTempo` left it at full) to 140 with it full, straight between.
- **In the game** (`TMoonGame`): `TickEclipse` steps the scene (`StepEclipse`) or brakes into it, then ticks the rite (`THeroOrbs.TickMoonRite`) after the hero is put where he hangs, so the ring keeps to him without trailing a tick. The catch holds him, plans the lift and calls `THenshin.StartEclipse`: the shout of the arenas before it (**EVOLUTION** and `evolution.wav` twice, `THenshin.Shout`), then the rite (`Calls.BeginMoonRite`). `Lifted` pauses the track, `DrawIn` tells the rite. `ClearEclipse` clears the rite as well.
- **The ceremony hears it** (`THenshin.HearMoonRite`): `mvDrawingIn` - the body fills with light (`ChargeLook`, moved to `Game.Shroud`, with the rite's collapse ticks); `mvFinished` - +3 health (`SuitCures`, the ice form's three) and `hfMoon`.
- **The moon form** (`THero.HeroForm` = `hfMoon`): in 3.0 it looks like the ice form and jumps +25% as it does (`SuitForms` in `Hero`). The burst, its messages and the suit's look on the strike are 16e's; till then he hangs in the beam with the dark in.
- **Tests**: `Tests.Orbs.Moonrite` (new, 19: it stands till started; sixty seats; one at a time at an even gap between beats; a beat shortens the gap; never shorter than the last; every orb sits, half on each ring, full once; not told, it stands ringed; the gathered only grow; an orb off a face is born in the matter; none off a face near him, some from twice as far; every fourth out of the depth, a speck behind him; from all sides; the ring beats with the heart; keeps to the hero; never armed; told, they go into his body and it is over; out of thin air with no matter; a collapse tells nothing; a clear takes it all). `Tests.Game.Eclipse` rewritten (20: the ring handed in as the game hands it - lifted once as the first orb sits and not before the catch; the dark follows the ring, keeps its pace, never lightens, goes all the way with the ring full; no beam till the ring is full; the draw-in once, the ring's time in the beam after the landing; the ripples from the lift till then). `Tests.Orbs.Patterns` +4 (the eclipse found by its word; two rings in front of the chest; its waves fill them together, the outer seats between the inner; the rings turn against each other). `Tests.Orbs.Harvest` +6 (`HarvestAfar`: its count; none near him; from all sides; from far and less far; no spot twice, by turn; thin air past its nearest). `Tests.Game.Heartbeat` +6 (the rite starts where the charge left it, 140 with the ring full, straight between, held outside; the ticks since a beat, none for a still heart). `TFarPlaneReadTests.TestUnderTheEmblemTheCityGivesItsPointsOnTheScreen`. The flights, the swell, the specks out of the city, the ripples, the shout over the gauge and the moon form's jump: checked live.
- First guesses for the live run: the gaps and the step on a beat, the share out of the depth, the flights' length and turn, the specks' size, the ring's radius and its draw-in on a thump, the ripples' reach, speed and level, the dark's pace against the ring, the ring's time in the beam, the drawing in, the fade of the track.

## Stage 16d reworked (3.0.104)
- **Decided 2026-11-06**: the hero is not held through the rite. From the brake to the throw he runs, jumps and shoots as ever, unwounded; the ring, the beam and the ripples keep to him wherever he goes - they were drawn about him every tick already. The catch and the lift (`Game.Hoist`, `THero.Hold`) wait for the throw (18a), where the orbs take him up before the pads; no code calls them till then.
- **The hull stays passable** (decided 2026-11-06): halted, the Ram neither touches nor throws, so the hero runs through it. A wall would ask for a hull to jump onto and run along, a mechanic the game has not and does not want.
- **The bonus is shut** through the scene (`ActivateQueuedBonus`, `FEclipse.Spares`): no ice aura or fire rain in the moon form's rite; a click is let go, as one held through the hold of 16c-2 was.
- **The scene** (`Game.Eclipse`): `Holds` and `Hoisted` are gone; `CatchAt` is `CallAt` and the cue `Caught` is `Called` - the world at rest, the rite called (`THenshin.StartEclipse`); `Lifted` is `FirstSeat` - the track begins to fade, the dark and the ripples begin. `TMoonGame.CatchHero` and the field `FHoist` are gone; `HullBox` stays for the rite's matter. `ClearEclipse` releases no hold.
- **The seats by turn** (`TMoonRite.NearestFreeSeat`): the k-th call aims a golden angle (137.5 degrees) on from the one before, from a first turn the dice pick, and takes the free seat whose turn is nearest that aim as the rings stand that tick; the face is then picked by the seat's turn as before. In 3.0.103 the k-th call took place k x 23 of the figure (`SpreadStride`, gone): with the outer ring turning against the inner, the turns of two places a stride apart close in as the rings run, and two calls one after another came from one side (`TestTheCallsComeFromAllSides`).
- **The gap from each call** (`RunCalls`): the wait starts anew from every call. 3.0.103 carried the part of a tick left over; once a beat had shortened the gap, the carry brought the next call on early, under the last gap (`TestTheGapIsNeverShorterThanTheLast`). A gap now lasts its whole ticks, rounded up.
- **Tests**: `Tests.Game.Eclipse` 18 (the hold's test and the lift's count gone; the call once as the road stands, nothing till an orb sits, the first seat told once and not before the call).

## The heads' tops as drawn (3.0.105)
- **Decided 2026-11-06**: bullets flew through the visible top of the hunter and of the bunker - unfair. Every head's top takes a bullet where the art draws it, measured on the art of `tek-machine.mset`.
- **The hunter** (`StrikeHunter`, was `StrikeEye`; the game's `BulletStruckRamHunter`, was `BulletStruckRamEye`): the eye as before (`EyeShare`, a circle of 5 about the lens) or the top - from the head's upper edge (`HunterTop`, 16 points, the art's frame) down to the lens's line (`HunterLensY` 8.8), whichever the stretch meets first. A bullet onto it from above or from the side takes a life; one that climbs across the lens's line into it has come up through the head and flies on, as through the rest of the head, the mast and the barrel.
- **The bunker** (`StrikeBunker`): the top is the dome from its upper edge (`DomeTop`, 14 points, the left-facing frame) down to the row of cells under the hatch (`OpenTop`, `HatchY` + `OpenRowsDepth`), and the lid wherever it stands (`InLid`: the point taken back through the lid's turn and way, as `DrawLid` moves it, to where it lies shut, and asked against `LidTop` and `LidBottom`, 13 and 5 points on the art as drawn facing right). At `HatchFallLeast` 30 degrees or steeper - a life; shallower or level - the honeycomb flares. Under the line the honeycomb as before. In 3.0.104 the top was the line itself within the hatch's width (`HatchHalfWidth` 8.5, gone): a bullet through the dome's shoulders or the shut lid, which stands about 6 units over the dome, flew on.
- **Decided 2026-11-06, after the run**: with the whole top a target the bunker was far too easy. The honeycomb stays as it is - its rows go 5.1 apart, so "a little higher" is a whole row back over the top, the unreadable top taken off in 3.0.98 - and the steepness goes from 15 degrees to 30: a shallow shot from a pad far off flares the honeycomb.
- **The walk** (`TopShare`): the stretch in the head's own units (`ToHead`, now of the head on the mast, not the sweeper only), every `StrikeStep` 0.5; the first point on the top. A climbing stretch that starts on the top, or crosses the top's lower line (`TopLine`) into it, meets it from inside: no hit. A turn squashed under `ThinHeadSquash` leaves no top (the hunter's eye stays). `OutlineAt` - the height of an edge across, between its two points.
- **Tests**: `TestOnlyTheEyeTakesAHit` became `TestTheEyeAndTheTopTakeAHit` (up through the head beside the eye - nothing; down onto the top beside it - a life, met over the eye; level at the lens - a life). `TRamBunkerTests` +2: `TestTheShutLidTakesALife` (straight down, stopping short of the dome), `TestTheShouldersBesideTheHatchTakeALife` (straight down past the hatch on either side). `TestARowOffTheTopReachesFromLower` became `TestOnlyASteepBulletTakesALife` (16 degrees - the honeycomb, 32 - a life); the shots down from a pad go at 35, 45 and 60 degrees in place of 25, 40 and 60.

## Stage 16e as built (3.0.106)
- **One burst, two strengths** (`Orbs.Burst`): the burst stays one for every form, the form lending its colors (`TBurstLook`); how strong it is the caller says (`TBurstPower`, a parameter of `TOrbBurst.Start`): rays, speed, tail, reach, the ring's run and time, the inhale, whether the inhale is bare (the veil and the figure), the flash's hold, time and level. `SuitBurst` is the burst of arenas 1 and 2 to the number (24 rays, 20 a tick, a tail of 170, gone at 590, a ring of 250 over 13 ticks, an inhale of 2, bare, a flash of 4 ticks at 0.5). `EclipseBurst`: 36 rays every 10 degrees, the first straight up; 26 a tick, a tail of 340, gone at 980 - the screen's diagonal (640) and the tail, so from anywhere on the screen the whole ray leaves it; a ring of 420 over 16 ticks; an inhale of 16 ticks, not bare; the flash at 0.95, held 2 ticks, dying over 3.
- **The moon look** (`MoonBurstLook`): a white heart along the head of a ray over its black body (`Heart`, `HeartLevel` 0.9; the band `HeartRayBands`, strong at the head alone - the share to the 4th), so the ray is a white core, a black tail and a light halo; its flash is white. The ice look has no heart (`HeartLevel` 0). The U key draws the new look at the suit's strength.
- **The inhale is the silence**: not bare - no veil, no figure: the dark holds, the beam holds, the hero runs in his own shape (`Inhaling` is the inhale of a bare burst; `Holding` - the world stands - is any inhale). The burst's center follows him through it and stays where the strike finds him, in a jump or on a pad as well, as in arenas 1 and 2.
- **The heart** (`Game.Heartbeat`): `StrikeLast` - the next tick strikes the last beat (`Final`), at `LastBeatStrength` 1.6 of an ordinary pulse (the beam swells with it); no beat after it, whatever tempo it is handed, till a stop; it keeps `Running`, so the monitor, paced, runs flat through the inhale. The game plays the last beat's sound twice at once. At the strike the game stops the heart: the monitor beats on its own again; the throw (18a) starts it anew.
- **The order** (`Game.Henshin`, `Game.Moon`): the rite's orbs gone into him (`mvFinished`), the ceremony asks the game for the eclipse's burst (`THenshinCalls.BurstEclipse`, the game's `BurstEclipse`: `THeroOrbs.BurstEclipse` and the heart's last beat). The burst inhales 16 ticks and strikes; the ceremony hears it (`FBurstForm` in place of `FBursting`: the form the burst it asked for puts on) and `FinishEclipse`: +3, `hfMoon`, `bottle.wav` twice at once, a shake of `EclipseTrauma` 1.0 (the suit's 0.7), then `THenshinCalls.TearDark`. The moon form and its cures moved from the end of the rite to the strike, as the ice suit goes on at its burst's strike.
- **The light back** (`TMoonGame.TearDark`, `TEclipseScene.Strike`): the dark is 0 at once and so is the tick before it, so not a frame of it is drawn; the beam, its flash and the ripples gone; the gauge out for good (`Fade` 1 from the strike, it no longer follows the dark); the scene cues nothing more and still spares the hero (`Struck`, `Spares`) till its clear. The boss track stays paused for the takeoff (18c).
- **The rays strike nothing**: `THeroOrbs.BurstEclipse` launches the burst with no curl - the rite of the eclipse has its own figure, not the level's vortex - and `ResolveBurstHits` strikes along no stretch of it (`FBurstStrikes`); the suit's burst strikes as before.
- **The Ram thrown and stunned** (`TRam.Stun`, `htStunned`): from a stand or the brake (not in its hunt), thrown away from the hero's center as the strike finds it - to the left from his right, to the right from his left, stern first from its very middle - `StunThrow` 16 over `StunThrowTicks` 6, fast and slowing, the mast swinging with the shove; not held to the screen - from the left edge its stern goes half a cell off it. Then it stands. Stunned: `Halted` (no touch, no throw), `CanBrake` false; the lamps out at once; the eye flickers out of step (`FlickerEye`: dark at `StunDarkShare` 0.45 of the picks, else from its breath up to a flash, each held 1 to 5 ticks, by its own dice); smoke out of both wheel arches, over the top of each tyre (`ArchSmokePoints`, `ArchSmokeLook`), thick for 100 ticks, then a trickle of 0.35, drawn with the light, over the body.
- **The hero free at the strike** (decided 2026-11-06): beside the hull the Ram leaves him; in a jump the rays come out of him in the air; inside the halted hull he stays there - it is passable through the scene, and the lift of the throw (18a, `Game.Hoist`) draws him clear of it. Nothing wounds him from the brake to the wheel; the arena has no pit.
- **Tests**: `Tests.Orbs.Burst` +4 (the eclipse's inhale half a second with no figure; 36 rays every 10 degrees, one straight up; faster and farther than the suit's; from the room's corner every ray flies on past the screen's diagonal), its bench on `SuitBurst`; `Tests.Game.Heartbeat` +5 (the last beat on the next tick, told once; none after it; the strongest; a still heart strikes its last at once; a stop forgets it); `Tests.Game.Eclipse` +6 (the dark torn off in one tick; the gauge out for good; the beam and the ripples gone; no cue after; spares till the clear; no strike outside the scene); `TRamBrakeTests` +8 (half a cell away from him and stands; from its left to the right; from its middle stern first; fast then slower; none in its hunt; the lamps out; the eye out of step; no touch, no throw, no brake); `Tests.Game.HeroOrbs` +4 (the eclipse's burst holds the world with no figure; strikes nothing; the suit's after it strikes again; none over a corpse). The rays, the heart of a ray, the flash, the shake, the sounds, the flat monitor, the flicker and the smoke: checked live.
- First guesses for the live run: the rays' count, speed and tail, the ring, the heart's width and level, the flash's level and hold, the inhale's length, the last beat's strength and sound, the shake, the throw's length and pace, the flicker's dark share and holds, the smoke's thickness, place and time.

## Stage 18a-1 as built (3.0.107)
- **The scene** (`Game.Throw`, `TThrowScene`): started by the burst's strike (`TMoonGame.TearDark`), cleared with the eclipse (`ClearEclipse`: a restart, point A, the End key). It knows neither the pads, the Ram nor the orbs: it tells its cues (`Catch`, `Grip`, `SpinUp`, `SpunUp`), keeps its clock and plans the arc; `TMoonGame.TickThrow`, after `TickEclipse`, carries them out. Stages: `tsWaiting`, `tsCarrying`, `tsGripping`, `tsSpinning`, `tsSpunUp`.
- **The Ram drives off** (`TRam.Withdraw`): told at the strike, right after `Stun`; once its throw is over it drives to stand with its left edge a cell from the screen's (`RamPlaceLeft` 32), a cell of road left behind it - slow, faster, slow, at `WithdrawPace` 2 a tick on average, no shorter than `WithdrawLeastTicks` 30; the engine's rumble as it sets off, the brake's hiss at the end (the cues `Rumble`, `Hiss`). Stunned all the way: the lamps out, the eye flickering, the smoke. Nothing for a machine not stunned.
- **The catch** `CatchAfterTicks` 33 after the strike. `CatchHero`: `THero.Hold`; the controls are not his (the held keys not polled, `Fire` gives nothing, a click on the bonus dropped). The orbs carry him (`Game.Hoist`, rewritten: `PlanHoist(From, Place)`) out of the picture, to his place `HeroPlaceEdge` 48 off the right edge, his feet `HeroHover` 8 over the road: along the straight line, eased in and out, bowed up by `HoistBow` 16 in the middle, at `HoistPace` 3 a tick on average and no shorter than `HoistLeastTicks` 30. The drawing clear of an edge and of the hull of 3.0.102 is gone with the lift: the Ram drives the other way and its hull is passable.
- **Too few pads** (the risk "The throw with too few pads", decided 2026-11-06: a floor of 4, `ThrowLeastPads`). At the catch the pads some of which the screen shows (`PadsInSight`) are counted; the missing ones are called from the pool of the chase (`FreeDrifter`, 64 wide, else 32) to the spots `PlaceCalledPads` picks: the decks of the hunt's rows (`RowDecks`), every 16 across, 16 off the edges, 16 of room about the pads in sight, the hull where it drives to (`RamPlaceBox`) and the hero in his place, each the farthest from the rest. They fly in from beyond the nearer side edge (`SetAdriftComing`) over his way's ticks, so they are in as he is in his place. Too many: the `ThrowMostPads` 7 nearest him are taken, the rest stay on their jets.
- **The arc** (`PlanArc`): the places of the middles of the beams, round the Ram's middle where it drives to (`ArcMiddle`): from 105 degrees (up and left) over its top to 10 (right, a little over its middle), all as far from it - its top `ArcTopClear` 36 under the top of the screen, so the radius is 252 - evenly apart over the whole of it however many pads: as wide for a few as for many, room for the volley's run-up. At `ThrowMostPads` 7 two neighbours stand about 69 apart, so two long pads spinning do not meet. One pad - over it, at the arc's middle. The pads take the places by their turn about the Ram (`ArcOrder`): the most counter-clockwise the first place, so no two ways cross.
- **The orbs** (`Orbs.Grip`, `TOrbGrip`, in `THeroOrbs`; `Orbs.Threads` of the first build is gone): in his place, the orbs go out to the pads, the nearest first, every seat of one before the next - one a tick (`GripGapTicks` 1) out of the point of his body that faces the seat (`FacingPoint`), off him along the straight line to the seat wherever it is now, eased, bowed 8 to 32 to either side in the middle, in 10 ticks and one for every 12 units of the way, 30 at most, growing out of him. The seats: along the top and the bottom edge of the beam, one every `GripSpacing` 10 (3 a side on a short pad, 6 on a long one), turned with the pad (`OnBeam`). Seated, an orb clings and goes where the beam goes; `GripSize` 0.55 of an orb of the rite, swelling by 0.15 on a thump. None armed. A pad's last orb seated, it is gripped (`TickGrip` tells the game).
- **The pads held** (`Game.Wrecks`): a gripped pad is dozed and held as a wreck in its picture (`TPadWrecks.Hold`) - no jet's fire, no sparks, no bolts, no glow: the jets dead. It turns about the middle of its beam (`PadPivot`, `PadBeamDepth` 6.3 - the beam of `city-pad` and `city-pad-long` is 38 px of 103): the wreck's middle is that point, and the picture hangs `PivotDrop` under it, turned with the spin (`PictureAt`; a blast's wrecks have none). Then it glides (`Guide`) to its place on the arc over `GlideTicks` 132, slow, faster, slow. It spins at `HeldSpin`, which the game sets every tick, times its own share - 0.8 to 1.2 by `WreckVariety`, one in five against the rest. Held, it is not `Gone` and not `Flying`, and keeps its place among the wrecks till a clear; `Place` - the slot of the first gone wreck or a new one - is shared with `Launch`.
- **The spin**: every pad gripped (`PadHeld` from each), it runs up straight over `SpinUpTicks` 132 (4 s, the glide's length) to `SpunUpSpin` 24 degrees a tick; a thump speeds it by `SpinBeatSurge` 0.3 (`SpinRate`). With no pad it runs up at once.
- **The heart** beats anew from the catch (`TThrowScene.Beats`; the first tick strikes): `SpinTempo` 70 with the pads still to 150 spun up, straight between (`Game.Heartbeat`).
- **The hum** (`tools/sounds/spin.py`): `spinrise.wav` at `SpinUp`, 4 s and a release of 0.1 - two low buzzing tones climbing an octave from 55 Hz, beating faster, swelling from 0.3; `spinhold.wav` looped from `SpunUp` (`FSpinHum`, paused with the menu, stopped by the clear) - a second of the top, 110 Hz, its beat and throb whole hertz, so the loop has no seam.
- **The Ram's lights in alarm** (`TRam.LightsAlarm`): as strong as the spin has run up; stunned, its lamps and its eye blink together - lit for the first half of a blink, out (the eye at its low) for the second - once in `AlarmBlinkSlow` 20 ticks at the least alarm down to once in `AlarmBlinkFast` 6 at the most; with no alarm, the lamps out and the eye flickering as since 3.0.106. A stun starts with none. The honeycomb holds still.
- **The hint** (`TThrowPainter.DrawHint`): spun up, the mouse glyph of the bonus slot - moved out of `THudCharge` into `DrawMouseGlyph` (`Hud.Charge`), the slot drawing it at 1 - comes in over 8 ticks over his head, at twice the HUD's size, swelling by 0.2 on a thump and its right button lit from 0.45 to all by the pulse. Drawn after the marks, under the crosshair. The press does nothing yet (18a-2).
- After it nothing: the pads spin on the arc, the hum holds, the hint pulses, he hangs in his place.
- **Tests**: `Tests.Game.Throw` (new) - `TThrowSceneTests` 10 (nothing till started; caught once a second after the burst, held and beating from then; carried along his way, then in his place; the orbs go out once as he is there; the spin waits for every pad; it runs up once over four seconds and stays; the hint after it, coming in; still pads spin at nothing, a thump spins faster; no pad - the spin at once; a clear takes it all), `TArcTests` 8 (a place for every pad, all as far; from up and left over the top to the right; evenly apart, no two meet; on the screen; its top just under the top of the screen; as wide for a few as for many; one pad over the Ram; taken by turn), `TCalledPadTests` 5 (as many as missing; on the rows inside the screen; clear of what is taken and of each other; from beyond the nearer edge; none without room). `Tests.Orbs.Grip` (new) - `TOrbGripTests` 10 (one leaves his body a tick; seats on both edges, more on a long beam; gripped once each in their order; seated within the longest flight; seated, they turn with the beam and go where it goes; smaller than the rite's, swell on a thump; none armed; a death draws them in; a clear takes them), `TFacingPointTests` 2. `Tests.Game.Hoist` rewritten - `THoistPathTests` 6 (starts where caught; ends in his place and stays; the least time for a short way; longer at the pace; slow, fast, slow; bowed up in the middle); `THoistPlanTests` gone with the drawing clear. `TPadHeldTests` 7 in `Tests.Game.Wrecks` (rises a little and hangs; guided, it glides to its place; the picture turns about the beam; spins as told, each its own share; still till told; keeps its place while others go; a clear). `Tests.Game.Heartbeat` +4 (`SpinTempo`). `TRamBrakeTests` +5 (in alarm its lamps and its eye blink together; the stronger the alarm, the faster; a new stun starts with none; stunned, it drives off where told; not stunned, it does not). The pads called in, the pictures, the hum and the hint: checked live.
- First guesses for the live run: the wait before the catch, the Ram's pace, the hero's place and way, the orbs' pace, bow, size and seats, the glide's length, the arc's angles and its top, the most pads, the spin's top and its beat surge, the spin-up's length, the tempo, the hum, the alarm's blink, the hint, the floor of 4 and where the called pads stand.

## Stage 18a-2 as built (3.0.108)
- **The press** (`TThrowScene.Press`): the right button - the one the hint shows - is the press while he is held (`TMoonGame.ActivateQueuedBonus`: held, the click goes to the scene and buys no bonus). Heard spun up only; a press while the pads spin up is dropped. With none, the pads go by themselves `ThrowAfterTicks` 99 (3 s) after the hint (`tsSpunUp` -> `tsVolley`, the cue `Volley`).
- **The silence**: from the volley the heart beats no more (`Beats` is the hold's till the volley; the heart stops as after the burst, the monitor on its own); the hum is cut (`BeginVolley`: `spinhold.wav` stopped); the hint gone.
- **Let go each at its own delay** (`Cue.LetGo`, `LetGoDelay`): pad `n` at `Round(LetGoMostTicks * Frac(n * 0.618))` ticks, `LetGoMostTicks` 6 (0.2 s) - the first at once, no two neighbours close. Till then it spins on held; the Ram's lights blink in alarm through the flight (`Spin` is 1 in `tsVolley`).
- **The flight** (`TPadWrecks.Hurl`, `TickHurled`): the wrecks' flight of 10c-3, aimed. The held pad is let go at the middle of the hull (`HullMiddle`), its jet burning again (fire, sparks, glow, bolts), its orbs riding it (option A, decided 2026-11-06). Kicked off at `HurlKick` 2, pushed `HurlThrust` 0.45 a tick toward the mark against the wrecks' drag 0.03 and the moon's pull 1/7 - up to about 11 a tick, there in about a second. It sets off bowed aside, always to the side that heads higher, by `HurlSwerveLeast` 15 to 40 degrees (`WreckVariety`, salt 6), the bow `HurlSwerveFade` 0.92 of itself a tick later: no two take one way and none flies a straight line. Its picture spins on at the rate it spun held at the let-go. On a model of all arcs of 1 to 7 pads, the hull taken as its box: every pad meets it in 28 ticks at the most, none lower than about 285 (the road at 352).
- **The jet from the picture** (`TPadWreckPainter.JetPoint`, the glow): from `PictureAt`, not the point it turns about - a held pad's picture hangs `PivotDrop` under its beam. A blast's wrecks have no drop, so for them nothing changed.
- **The hit** (`TMoonGame.StrikeHoneycomb`, first in `TickThrow`): each pad let go meets the honeycomb on its way of the last tick as a bullet does (`TRam.Block` - the entry along the stretch, the flare there). There (`BlowPad`): the wreck gone (`Smash`), its orbs burnt out where they cling (`TOrbGrip.Burn`), a piece of the honeycomb broken off (`TRam.ShatterShield`), a machine's blast (`ekMachine`), `platform.wav`, a shake of `ExploderTrauma` 0.4. The last: the boss's blast (`ekBoss`), `bottle.wav`, `platform.wav` and `sweeperblast.wav` together - the sweeper's death - a shake of `BossBlastTrauma` 1.0, and the honeycomb crumbles. A pad that missed and left the screen counts as struck with no blast, its orbs out - the scene and the crumble do not wait for it. No pad at all: the honeycomb crumbles at the volley.
- **The honeycomb broken** (`Game.Shield`): `TTekShield.Shatter` - every cell within `ShatterReach` 12 of the blow is gone; those of the rim fly off as shards away from it (0.8 to 2.4 a tick, 0.6 up, falling 0.08 a tick faster, burning out over 24 ticks; drawn under the cells). It still holds. `Crumble` (the state `sdCrumbling`): it holds no more; the rest blinks (3 ticks lit, 3 dark, at half a flare's light) and every cell goes out `CrumbleWait` 4 ticks in and a tick later for every `CrumblePace` 3 units it lies from the nearest piece broken off - from the middle when none is; the last seen cell out, it is down. `Rise` makes it whole and forgets the breaks: a restart, point A and the End key raise it as before. The cannons' fall is as it was.
- **The music back** (`CrumbleHoneycomb`, the one home of the crumble - the last hit, the last pad missing, no pad at all): the boss track starts again from its beginning (`ChangeMusic`, fading in over 1.5 s - the blast first), not resumed where the rite paused it. A placeholder: a track of its own for this moment is planned (see "The throw").
- After it the scene stands (`tsStruck`): he hangs in his place, the controls not his, till the wheel (18b); the Ram stunned, its lamps out, its eye flickering, the smoke.
- **Tests**: `Tests.Game.Throw` - `TThrowSceneTests` +6 (the press heard spun up only; with none, three seconds after the hint; the heart stops at the volley, he hangs on; each pad let go once within its delay, not all on one tick; every pad struck, the scene stands; no pad - all struck at once), `TestTheHintComesInSpunUp` ticks only till the throw. `Tests.Game.Wrecks` - `TPadHurledTests` 6 (new: from every place of every arc of 1 to 7 pads it passes within 50 of the mark within 40 ticks, on the screen; bowed aside at least 4 before it closes in; no two take one way; spins on as it spun held; smashed, gone; a wreck not held is not hurled). `Tests.Orbs.Grip` +1 (a pad blown up burns out its own orbs and no other's). `Tests.Game.Shield` (new) - `TShieldCrumbleTests` 6 (a blow breaks a piece, it still holds; crumbled, it holds no more and a blow does nothing; out from the breaks outward - longer from an end than from the middle; no break, from the middle; risen, whole again, the old breaks forgotten; a falling shield does not crumble). The flight's look, the fire, the blasts, the shards, the blinking and the sounds: checked live.
- First guesses for the live run: the 3 s, the delays, the kick, the thrust, the swerve and its fade, the blasts' kinds and shakes, the shards' reach, speed and life, the crumble's pace, wait, blink and glow, the sounds.

## Stage 18b as built (3.0.109)
- **The cue** (`Game.Throw`): the game tells the scene the honeycomb is down (`HoneycombDown`, heard from the volley on; `TTekShield.State` `sdDown`); `tsStruck` -> `tsBare`, and `WheelAfterTicks` 16 later `tsLoose` with the cue `Shed`. `Holds` is the catch till `tsLoose`; the scene stands in `tsLoose`, cueing nothing more.
- **The wheel off** (`TRam.ShedWheel`, `TMoonGame.ShedWheel`): stunned only, once a fight; the bow's wheel (`FrontAxle` 0 - the right one, facing right). `crash.wav`, `scrape.wav`, a shake of `WheelShedTrauma` 0.5. The hero is let go (`THero.Release`: down onto the road from 8 over it), the controls his again; the eclipse spares him no more (`TEclipseScene.StopSparing`; a new start spares him again).
- **The lean**: about the rear wheel's touch of the road (`LeanPivot`, `RearAxle` 1), `LeanDegrees` 3 over `LeanTicks` 6, falling faster (the square of the share), toward the way the hull faces. `THullStand` carries it (`Lean`, `Pivot`, `Shed` - the axles lost; `Turned`, `TurnedCenter`), and `Monsters.Hull` turns every layer about it: the chassis, the wheels left, the hull, its worn copy, its eye; `TRamPainter` the well, the Ram's eye, the lamps, the arch smoke and the exhausts (`LeanSpot`). The mast is not drawn while the hull leans: its clips are level lines, and at rest it is out of sight anyway (the risk "The hull's lean" - there is no stump to show). `TMonster.HullStand` starts from `Default`.
- **The scrape**: the point of the plough (`NosePoint` 2, 127.5 of the art) digs about 7 under the road line. Sparks off it for `ScrapeTicks` 30, dying out (`ScrapeLevel`): 5 a tick at the strongest, 25 degrees up ahead of the bow in a cone of 50, 1.5 to 6 a tick, ringing off the road (`TRamPainter.TickScrape`, `FScrape`). `scrape.wav` (`tools/sounds/scrape.py`): a band of noise wavering as the point catches and slips, the hull's plates ringing low, a knock as the nose lands; 1.3 s.
- **The wheel** (`Game.Wheel`, `TLooseWheel`, held by the Ram - `TRam.Wheel` - and drawn over the hull by `THull.DrawWheel`): at the axle's spot, the art's size (radius 20.5); hops at `WheelHopKick` 2.5 a tick under the moon's pull 2/14 - about 22 over the road - and comes down once (`TRamCue.WheelLands`, `wheelland.wav`: a low thump and a short ring of the rim); rolls right at `WheelRollPace` 3 a tick, turning by its way, gone once past the right edge. Its touch (`ResolveWheelContact`): `HurtByRam` and a throw away from its middle, straight up where the screen's edge leaves no room (`AwaySide`).
- **The hull's touch** wounds and throws again from the wheel on (`TRam.Contact`: halted, it is a scene till then).
- **The moon form's shout** (a side mission): at the strike of the eclipse's burst (`THenshin.FinishEclipse`) the ticker "MOON FORM: jump +25%" and the big "Moon Form" ("Форма Луны"), as the ice form's (`SMoonForm`, `SMoonFormPerk`).
- After it the scene stands: the Ram stunned and leaning, he free on the road, till the takeoff (18c).
- **Tests**: `Tests.Game.Wheel` (new) - `TLooseWheelTests` 8 (nothing till it is off; hops once, told once, then on the road; right at an even pace; clockwise by its way; gone once past the right edge; touches a body in its way, not one over it; away from its middle, straight up at the edge; a stop takes it). `TRamBrakeTests` +6 (stunned, the bow's wheel off once; not stunned, none; leans onto its bow and stays; its touch wounds again; the wheel comes down, told once, and rolls away; a new fight puts it back, the hull level). `Tests.Game.Throw` +3 (the wheel a breath after the honeycomb is down, not before; let go at the wheel, the scene stands; the honeycomb's fall heard from the volley on). `Tests.Game.Eclipse` +1 (told so, it spares him no more till a new start). `Tests.Monsters.Hull` +2 (a level stand turns nothing; a leaning one turns its points about the pivot). The lean, the dig, the sparks, the wheel's look and the sounds: checked live.
- First guesses for the live run: the breath before the wheel, the lean's degrees and drop, the nose's point, the sparks, the shake, the hop, the roll's pace, the sounds.

## The bare hull's ricochet as built (3.0.110)
- **Decided 2026-11-07**: the bare hull and the wheel throw bullets off as the tank's armour does, and take no damage yet.
- **While the honeycomb holds** it takes the bullet into its light (`TRam.Block`), as before. **Once it is not up** (`TTekShield.State` past `sdUp`) the hull itself does (`TRam.StrikeHull`): the stretch of the bullet's tick is turned back into the upright hull about the rear wheel's touch, walked in steps of 0.5 over the body's grid (`TShieldMask`, mirrored as the hull faces), and the first solid cell is traced back to the edge the bullet came in through (`TraceEntry` of `Game.Impacts`, as a box of armour); the point and the normal are turned out again with the lean.
- **The wheel shed** (`TLooseWheel.Strike`, `TRam.StrikeWheel`): where the stretch first meets its circle, the normal out of its middle; a stretch begun inside meets it where it began.
- **The sparks** (`TMoonGame.BulletStruckRam`): the bullet spent, `FImpacts.Land` with its speed and the normal - the fan glancing off as off a mirror, now and then a tracer with `ricochet.wav`, else an armour ping (`SoundArmorHit`). A hit within `RapidHitTicks` 8 of the last (`HullStruckWithin`, the hull and the wheel together) is rapid: no flash of its own.
- **Tests**: `Tests.Game.Wheel` +3 (a bullet meets its rim facing it; one over it or short of it nothing; none with no wheel off) - and its fixture registered, missing in 3.0.109; `TRamBrakeTests` +4 (while the honeycomb holds the hull throws nothing off; bare, off its face; leaning, off its top, turned toward the bow; the wheel throws one off, a hit of the hull, forgotten after a while). The sparks and the sounds: checked live.

## Stage 18c-1 as built (3.0.111)
- **The cue** (`Game.Throw`): from `tsLoose` the game tells the scene once the front wheel has rolled off the screen (`WheelGone`: shed and not running); `tsGone`, `TakeoffAfterTicks` 20 later `tsTakenOff` with the cue `TakeOff`, and the game calls `TRam.TakeOff` (only with the front wheel off, once a fight). The scene then stands.
- **The motion** (`Game.Takeoff`, `TTakeoff`, held by the Ram - `TRam.Flight`): the front jet catches at the first tick, and the lean goes on a smooth step from the 3 of 18b to `BowUpDegrees` 10 the other way round (bow up, about the rear wheel's touch) over `BowUpTicks` 24. `RearJetAfterTicks` 33 after the start the rear jet catches and spits the rear wheel out (`Spit`); from there `tkRising`: the lift goes to `HangLift` 184 on a smooth step over `RiseTicks` 75 (the bottom of the body about 150 from the top of the screen, just over the moon form's jump), and the hinge moves off the wheel's touch to the middle of the hull over `AirTicks` 20 (`AirAt`). All the while a damped spring pulls the lean to level (`SpringPull` 0.0204, `SpringDrag` 0.1: a swing of about 44 ticks, about 3 degrees past level): it settles about 100 ticks after the spit, `Steady`, `tkHanging`, level. A jet flares by half of itself as it catches over 3 ticks and comes down to its steady fire over 10 (`JetAt`).
- **The pose** (`TRamPose`, `TRam.PoseAt`): upright on the road, turned `Lean` about the rear wheel's touch, moved by `Shift`. Off the road the shift carries the turn about the middle (the middle minus where the turn about the touch takes it, times `AirAt`) and the lift. `Posed` puts a point of the upright hull where the pose has it, `PoseSpot` a point of the art. In place of `LeanSpot`: the eye, the lamps, the exhausts, the arch smoke, the nose's sparks, the smoke over the empty mast and the hull's layers (`THullStand.Center` and `Pivot` moved by the shift) all go with the pose; off the upright the mast is not drawn, as in the lean.
- **Touch and bullets**: `TRam.Overlaps` and `SpanAcross` bring the hero's box back into the upright hull (`UprightBox`: its corners un-posed, their bounding box - a little wider while it leans); `StrikeHull` brings the bullet's stretch back the same way and poses the point it meets. Hanging, the hull wounds by touch up there and not on the road; a bullet meets its raised top. Since the lean now counts in the touch, the touch in 18b's 3 degrees counts it too.
- **The rear wheel** (`TRam.RearWheel`, `SpitWheel`): out of the rear axle's spot on the road (the bow up about that wheel's touch, it has hardly moved), hopping at `WheelSpitKick` 4 - about 56 over the road (`TLooseWheel.Start` takes the kick) - then rolling right as the first does: `WheelLands` once more, its touch wounds and throws (`ResolveWheelContact` tries both), a bullet ricochets off it (`StrikeWheel` tries both). The rear axle drawn shed from `tkRising` on.
- **The fire and the sound** (`TRamPainter`): a jet out of the middle of each well, where the axle was - `TSmoke` with a fire look straight down (`JetFireLook`: 140 a second, 0.4 s, at 150 a second, a cone of 14), drawn with the smoke before the body; a glow and a white core at the mouth, 12 under the axle, swelling with the flare (`DrawJetGlow`, in the light pass); dust off the road under a jet whose mouth is within `JetReach` 70 of it (`JetDustLook`). Each jet catching: the cue `Ignite`, `jetfire.wav`, a shake of `IgniteJolt` 0.35. At the spit the cue `Hover`: the rumble's channel takes the loop `jethover.wav`. Both from `tools/sounds/jet.py`: the fire - a low thump as the fuel takes, a roar swelling past its steady level and settling, a hiss over it, 1.6 s; the hover - the roar and the hiss in a seamless loop of 2 s, breathing three times a loop.
- After it the Ram hangs where it rose, level, its jets burning, till 18c-2 moves it over the hero.
- **Tests**: `Tests.Game.Takeoff` (new) - `TTakeoffTests` 9 (nothing till it starts; the front jet at the first tick; the bow up against the way it faces, nothing risen; the rear jet once, with the wheel, a while after; up to its height, never down; past level and settled, told once, level; about the wheel on the road, about the middle off it; a jet's flare then its steady fire; a stop takes it all). `TRamBrakeTests` +6 (with its wheels on, no takeoff; once - two jets, the roar once, both wheels down; hanging, level at its height; hanging, its touch up there, not on the road; hanging, a bullet meets its raised top, not where it stood; a new fight puts it back on the road). `Tests.Game.Throw` +2 (the wheel gone, a breath, the takeoff once, the scene stands; told before the wheel is off, not heard). `Tests.Game.Wheel` +1 (a spat one hops higher, down once). The fire, the dust, the swing and the sounds: checked live.
- First guesses for the live run: the bow's 10 degrees and its pace, the breath before the rear jet, the rise's pace and height, the spring's swing, the spit's hop, the fire's look and reach, the dust, the shakes, the sounds' levels.

## After the live run of 3.0.111 (decided 2026-11-07)
- **The rise is good enough.** Hanging it stood still - it is to rock.
- **The engine is done with at the takeoff**: the exhausts smoke no more from then on (the rumble had gone at the brake already; the jets' roar stays).
- **A hatch shuts the well** once the third head is gone - its own small thing, built with 18c-2.
- **The fire did not convince**: two candles under a heavy hull. Four ways were weighed (nozzles out of the arches, a skirt of plates with nozzles under the belly, side turbines, anti-gravity discs); chosen: **nozzles out of the arches**. A first sketch hung bell nozzles out of the dark arches - it read as stuck on, too big, and the bell not technical enough. The way taken, after how it is done elsewhere (the F-35B's swivel nozzle behind doors on stacked rotating collars, the Harrier's nozzles turned by a visible drive, the tilting thrusters of Halo's Pelican and Thunderbird 2's jets tucked under its sides): the arch becomes an **engine bay** - a heat shield of hexagonal tiles at the top, a ribbed duct down the middle into a segmented swivel ring on the wheel's axle, two hydraulic actuators from the walls to the ring, armoured bay doors folded open against the walls, a riveted bulkhead closing the bottom - and under the ring a **short nozzle of steel petals** with a hazard collar and heat-tinted lip, turned by code with the hull's rock. The bay is darkened, most at the top, so it reads as recessed; the nozzle is not. Both generated (2026-11-07), one bay on both arches. The fire narrower and shorter than in 3.0.111, fading well over the road; only a warm hint reaches it.
- **Stages, as few as can be**: 18c-2 - the hang (rock, bob, after the hero), the exhausts out, the well's hatch; 18c-3 - the bay and the nozzles (their sliding out at the takeoff), the new fire. **Point B moves to 17a**: in the hang nothing can kill him yet, and point B is to give him the orb gun, which comes in 17a.

## Stage 18c-2 as built (3.0.112)
- **The hang** (`Game.Takeoff`, stage `tkHanging`): from the moment it settles the bob (`BobUnits` 3 over `BobTicks` 80) and the rock (`WobbleDegrees` 1.2 over `WobbleTicks` 110) grow in over `SwayRampTicks` 33, so the settled hull does not jump. `Tick` takes how far the hero is across (`AToward`, from the middle of the hull); hanging, a spring pulls toward him and a drag holds back (`ChaseSpring` 0.004, `ChaseDrag` 0.12), the pace changing by no more than `ChaseAccel` 0.06 a tick and never past `ChaseMaxPace` 1.6 (he runs at 2): from 200 away it comes over him in about 200 ticks, without overshooting. It banks into the way it goes, `BankPerPace` 2.5 degrees clockwise a unit a tick to the right - about 4 at full pace. Rising, it hears none of it.
- **In the Ram** (`TRam.TickTakeoff`): the hero's middle, kept within half a hull of the screen's edges, less the hull's middle; the hull moves across by `Pace` (`FX`), so everything that goes with the pose goes with it.
- **The exhausts** (`TRamPainter.TickExhausts`): off from the takeoff's start (`Flight.Running`), fading as they fade.
- **The well's hatch** (`TRam.ShutterAt`, `TickShutter`, `ShutterTicks` 20): it starts sliding once the bunker is gone and the mast has sunk (`TickChange`, the bunker's branch), and stays shut; past the bunker (point A) it is shut from the start; a new fight opens it (`Place`). Drawn over the hull after the mast (`DrawShutter`): the sprite `wellShutter` centred on the well, cut by a level line moving from the far edge of the mouth toward the eye while it shuts - it shuts with the hull upright on the road. The smoke out of the empty mast stops once it is shut.
- **The art**: a generated round hatch seen from above and in front, on a white ground (`machine-hatch.png` among the sources, kept out of the repository as the build's are: a riveted rim, a hazard ring, four ribs to a hub with a locking wheel, two slide brackets, the thick side band of the lid), turned into `wellShutter` by `tools/machine/extend_machine.py`, run on the built set (the build's own sources are not needed): the white cleared from the edges inward, its top face squashed to the mouth's ellipse and a little wider than the mouth, so its own rim sits on the well's and its side band shows under it (206 x 55 px, `ShutterWidth` 34.3 x `ShutterHeight` 9.2 units, the middle of the face the middle of the sprite). The tool puts a sprite in place of one of the same name, so it runs again as is. Tried before it: a hatch from straight above squashed flat, and the plating of the hull itself.
- **Tests**: `TTakeoffTests` reworked for the hang (to its height as it settles; level as it settles) and +3 (bobs and rocks a little about its height and level; after the hero - from rest slowly, never past its most, banked into the way, coming to rest over him; rising, deaf to him). `TRamBrakeTests`: level at its height as it settles, +1 (goes over the hero no faster than its most, on the screen). `TRamBunkerTests` +2 (after the bunker the well shuts only once the mast has stopped, never opening; past the bunker shut from the start). The rock, the exhausts and the hatch: checked live.
- First guesses: the bob and the rock, the chase's spring, drag and pace, the bank, the hatch's pace and its look.

## Stage 18c-3 as built (3.0.113)
- **The order** (`Game.Takeoff`, a stage `tkOpening` before `tkBowUp`): the cue `TakeOff` no more lights the front jet at once. The bays come out of both arches over `BayTicks` 14, then the nozzles slide out of their rings over `NozzleTicks` 8 (`BayAt`, `NozzleAt`, smooth steps, never back, both arches alike), the hull as it leaned in 18b; on the tick the nozzles are out the front jet catches (`Ignite`) and the bow-up of 18c-1 starts. The rear bay and nozzle come out with the front ones, behind the rear wheel (drawn under it), so the rear jet still spits the wheel out `RearJetAfterTicks` 33 after the front one catches. The takeoff is 22 ticks longer.
- **The art** (`tools/machine/extend_machine.py`, which now takes all three of its pictures out of one sources dir: `machine-hatch.png`, `machine-bay.png`, `machine-nozzle.png`): `engineBay` - the opening of each arch read off the hull itself (the clear run of each row through the axle's column, up while the column is clear, down till the run leaks out under the feet), both openings laid on their axles into one mask, grown 3 px under the frame's edge but not below the feet; the bay scaled to the mask's width (4% over), its bottom on the feet's line, the ring's middle across on the axle - the ring then starts just under the axle (its swivel stack about 1 to 9 units under it); darkened from half its light at the top of the opening to three quarters at the bottom; cut by the mask; the axle at the middle of the sprite (251 x 217 px, `BayWidth` 41.8 x `BayHeight` 36.2). `engineNozzle` - the petal nozzle as wide as the ring less 3% (84 x 95 px, `NozzleWidth` 14 x `NozzleHeight` 15.8), hung by the middle of its top edge from the ring's open bottom, `NozzleHang` 8.9 under the axle; not darkened. `engineNozzleLit` - the nozzle warmed from below (nothing over 30% of its height, the most and a warm cast at the lip), clear at its top. A picture that comes clear already keeps its own alpha (`keyed`); the tool runs again on its own output as is.
- **The drawing** (`TRamPainter.DrawEngines`, between `THull.DrawChassis` and `THull.DrawWheels` - `Monsters.Hull` draws the two apart now): in each arch the bay grows out of the depth from `BayDepth` 0.6 of itself about the axle and out of the chassis' dark (its opacity the share out); the nozzle with the pose's lean, only its lower part out of the ring showing (`TSpriteRenderer.DrawHanging`: the lower share of a picture hung by its top edge and turned about it); the lit copy over it at the jet's level. On the road a nozzle slides out no deeper than `NozzleRoadSink` 1 under the road line (`NozzleOut`) - the front one, its hull on its bow, comes out about half and the rest as the bow lifts.
- **The fire** (`DrawFlames`, in `DrawSmoke`, before the body so the lip covers its root): no more smoke particles. One texture of a flame along its height (`CreateFlameShape`: the mouth's width, swelling a little, thinning to the tip, brightest at the mouth down the middle), drawn twice additively along the hull's own down out of the nozzle's mouth (`NozzleMouth`, `NozzleLipInset` 0.8 over its edge): an orange sheath `FlameLength` 30 by `FlameWidth` 8, a white-blue core `FlameCoreLength` 12 by `FlameCoreWidth` 3.2; longer with the flare (to 1.5), flickering by `FlameFlicker` 0.1, two waves beating, the rear jet out of step. Hanging, the tip ends about 150 over the road. In the light pass (`DrawJetGlow`): the glow at the mouth, smaller (`JetGlowSize` 22, a white-blue `JetCoreSize` 8), and the warmth on the road - a flat orange pool `WarmWidth` 70 by `WarmHeight` 12 under each jet, `WarmLevel` 0.5 with the mouth on the road, gone `WarmReach` 260 over it (about a sixth of it under the hanging hull). The dust as before, from a mouth within `JetReach` 40 of the road.
- **The wells' hollow** (the Risks' item of 3.0.111): `TRamArt.Wells` - the cells of the body that are the wheels and the arches' dark, not the hull (`ReadWells`); `TRam.Create` takes them (an overload without them keeps the wells solid). `SolidAt` leaves a well's cells out while its wheel is gone and its bay is not half out (`WellOpen`, `BayHolds` 0.5): between 18b and the takeoff a bullet goes into the bow's arch and a touch with it; from the bays on the arches are solid again - the bay fills them. The nozzle under the hull is not in the grid.
- **Tests**: `TTakeoffTests` +2 and reworked for the opening (the bays then the nozzles come out, never back, the hull as it leaned, no fire, out for good after; the front jet once the nozzles are out; the bow, the rear jet, the wheel's touch and the flare counted from there; nothing of them before the start or after a stop). `TRamBrakeTests` +1 (with the lower band as its wells: a level shot through the bow's arch meets the face with its wheels on, goes in as far as the rear arch with the front wheel shed, meets the face again once the bay is out). The bays, the nozzles' sliding, the lit copy, the fire and the warmth: checked live.
- First guesses for the live run: the opening's pace (14 + 8 ticks), the bay's depth, the darkening, the nozzle's hang and size, the fire's length, width and colours, the flicker, the glow at the mouth, the warmth's reach and level.

## The wheels' bounce and the held charge as built (3.0.114)
- **Asked 2026-11-07**: a shed wheel does not roll at once after its hop - it bounces, dying out, so it is harder to dodge; every strike of the road in sparks (the scrape's look), a sound and a small shake; both wheels, one physics. The rear one dropped from higher - how high, counted. And in the eclipse the body went dark between the orbs' drawing in and the blast: the charge's light died out over the collapse while the burst's inhale of 16 ticks still went on.
- **The bounce** (`Game.Wheel`): at every strike the wheel keeps `WheelBounceKeep` 0.6 of its speed up; a bounce slower than `WheelRestRise` 1.0 a tick and it rolls. `Struck` is the speed it struck at, on that tick only; `Tick` is True at every strike; `Contact` is the bottom of its rim. The front wheel: its hop to 22, bounces to about 10 and 5, then rolls from about x 380. `Start` lost its kick; `Drop` throws a wheel down from over the road onto the road line it is given, at `WheelSpitKick` 4 a tick.
- **The height, counted** (`Game.Takeoff`, `SpitLift` 48): the rear jet catches and the rise begins with the rear wheel still in its well (drawn and solid as before); at a lift of 48 - a cell and a half, a quarter of the climb, about 25 ticks into the rise - the jet throws it down out of the well (`Spit`, `Spat`; `TRam.SpitWheel` drops it from where the pose has the rear axle, onto the road under the upright axle). Its bottom then stands about 45 over the road (the lean takes a little). With the throw it strikes at about 5.5 a tick, at x about 100, under the hull, and bounces to about 40, 16 and 7, rolling from about x 400. Weighed (the first bounce's top for a drop from 32, 48, 64, 80: about 35, 41, 47, 52 - the jet's throw gives most of it): from 32 the fall hardly reads, the wheel hardly out of the arch; from 64 and up the hull is a third of its way up and more with the wheel still on - it reads as carried up, for a few units more of bounce. At 48 the first bounce tops out a little over the hero's height: from x 100 to 245 the wheel's bottom is over his head at the top of the arc only - running under it is a gamble, jumping it a matter of timing, and past it the low quick bounces want a jump again. The hover's roar comes at the throw, as it came at the spit.
- **The strike** (`TRam.HearStrike`): every strike shakes, `WheelStrikeTrauma` 0.2 at a strike of `WheelHardStrike` 5.5 and less as it softens; the cue `WheelLands` (`wheelland.wav`) only from `WheelThumpStrike` 2 a tick - the front wheel's hop and the rear one's first three; the small last ones are only seen. The sparks (`TRamPainter.SprayStrike`, `FWheelSparks`, the scrape's look `ScrapeSparkLook`, ringing off the road): up to `WheelSparks` 14 at a hard strike, fewer and slower as it softens, a fan of `WheelSparkCone` 150 up off the road both ways.
- **The charge held** (`Game.Shroud`): `TShroudLook.Hold`, the share of its life the peak is held for after `FlashAt`. `ChargeLook(ACollapseTicks, AInhaleTicks)`: with an inhale the climb is the same, and the top is held through the inhale and the tick of the strike (`AInhaleTicks` + 1), then falls as before; without one (the ice form's rite, its inhale of 2 covered by the bare figure) it is as it was. The moon rite's `mvDrawingIn` starts it with `EclipseBurst.InhaleTicks` (`THenshin.HearMoonRite`). At the moon numbers (30 and 16) the look lives 52 ticks, topping at 31.5 and falling from 48.5; the strike comes at 47 to 48.
- **Tests**: `Tests.Game.Wheel` reworked - `TLooseWheelTests` 13 (bounces lower and softer, then rolls on the road; told at every strike and only then, its rim on the road; a dropped one falls onto its road, strikes harder and bounces higher than a hopped one's hop; the old hop-once and spat-higher tests gone). `Tests.Game.Takeoff` - the rear jet's test reworked (the rear jet once, the wheel thrown out once after it, on the tick it is up `SpitLift`, out from then on); a stop puts the wheel back in. `TRamBrakeTests` +2 (every strike of the wheel jolts softer; the rear wheel falls out of the risen hull onto the road), both wheels down heard at least three times. `Tests.Game.Shroud` +2 (a charge climbs through the drawing in and falls, out before an inhale would end; with an inhale the climb is the same and the top is held through it to the strike). The bounce's look, the sparks, the shakes and the light: checked live.
- First guesses for the live run: the keep 0.6 and the rest 1.0, the drop's 48 and the throw's 4, the thump's threshold, the shake's 0.2, the sparks' count and fan.

## The flight's track as built (3.0.115)
- **The track**: `flight.ogg` (Suno, the author's; Vorbis, stereo, 48 kHz, 160 kbit/s, 3:09, about -14.7 LUFS against `boss3.ogg`'s -14.5 - as loud), in `bin/music/` since 2026-11-07. It is the track of the fight after the armour, "a track of its own for this moment" of "The throw".
- **When**: where the boss track used to start again - the fall of the honeycomb (`TMoonGame.CrumbleHoneycomb`, the one home of the crumble: the last hit, the last pad missing, no pad at all), from its beginning, fading in over 1.5 s under the blast, looping. The silence from the volley to the last hit is as it was; `boss3.ogg` plays no more after the honeycomb.
- **Where it is named**: the level's `ram` action, `"flightFile"` beside `"file"` (`TRamOrders.FlightTrack`, `TRam.FlightTrack`); screen 13's `s13-ram`: `"file": "boss3.ogg", "flightFile": "flight.ogg"`. With no `"flightFile"` the flight takes the fight's track, as before.
- **A death after it**: point A is before the henshin, so the boss track comes back there (`ResumePastBunker` starts the action's `"file"` when it does not play) and pauses at the rite as before; the honeycomb's fall brings `flight.ogg` in again.
- **Tests**: `TRamLevelTests` +1 (the flight's track read; none named - the fight's), level 2's arena names `flight.ogg`. The music itself: checked live.
- First guesses for the live run: the fade-in of 1.5 s under the blast, the loop's seam.

## Stage 17a as built (3.0.116)
- **The arrival** (`Game.OrbGun`, `TOrbGunArrival`): the game starts it every tick the hull hangs (`FRam.Flight.Stage = tkHanging`) and the hero lives; it starts once and holds. `ogWaiting` `OrbGunBreathTicks` 33, then the cue `Appear` and `ogAppearing` - the game starts the gun's shroud at its place; the gun's shroud done putting it together (`THeroShroud.Assembling` false), the cue `Lay` and `ogLying` - the pickup stands on the road; `Take` - `ogTaken`. A clear (`ClearEclipse`: a death, a new level) takes it all. Its place, `OrbGunSpotX(hero's middle)`: the middle of the screen, or `OrbGunClearance` 32 - the next cell - aside of him when he stands nearer, on the side the screen is wider.
- **Its shroud**: a second `THeroShroud` (`FOrbGunShroud`, made with the hero's in `LoadLevel`) started with `ReviveLook`, and a second `TShroudPainter` over the frame `lying` of `orbgun.mset`; its pose (`TMoonGame.OrbGunPose`) - frame 1, the cell `FOrbGunLeft` along, its feet on `RamFloorY`. Drawn with the monsters: under them `DrawUnder`, over them `DrawBody` and `DrawOver`. Till it is put together there is no pickup - the strips are the gun; then the pickup stands where they put it.
