- **The shroud's figure** (`Game.Shroud`): `TShroudPainter.Create(renderer, skin, frames, pose, shroud)` - the sprite set the frames are in, their names (frame 1 the first), `TShroudPose` (`reference to function: THeroPose`) - in place of `Create(renderer, hero, shroud)`; `HeroShroudFrames(skin)` - the hero's frames in the order of his poses. The hero's painter: `Create(FRenderer, FHero.SkinSet, HeroShroudFrames(...), FHero.Pose, FShroud)`. `THeroShroud` itself never knew the hero: unchanged. The name now says less than the class does - a rename is a restyle of its own.
- **The pickup** (`bin/monsters.json`, `weaponOrbGun`): `pickup`, `giveWeapon` - type 6, a cooldown of 4, bullets at 10, gravity 1 (the chain's fire a little faster, one orb a shot); "Орбовое ружьё активировано" / "Orb Gun acquired", `gun.wav`. Laid by `LayOrbGun` (`TMonsterField.SpawnStanding`, the set `orbgun`), its uptake picture made at once (`PictureOf`). Taken as any weapon - `WeaponUptake`.
- **The take** (`TMoonGame.TakeWeapon`, every weapon pickup goes through it): for the orb gun the gun he holds is put away first (`THero.StowGun`), then the pickup applies; then `FOrbGun.Take` and the hull's lives armed by the grade - the bar comes down and boots. A restart gives the put-away gun back (`THero.UnstowGun` in `ClearEclipse`).
- **The gun in the hands** (`Hero`): `OrbGunWeapon` 6 beside `DupletWeapon` 5; `orbgun.mset`, its own cache with alpha and linear filtering, `held` drawn `OrbGunHeldSide` 32 square; `DrawWeapon` picks the gun's picture by a `case`. `FireOrb`: one bullet of the look `blOrb`, `MuzzleReach` 22 along the shot from the 2008 spawn point - the bore on the line within 1.5. `TStowedGun` (`Held`, type, cooldown, speed, gravity).
- **The art** (`tools/orbgun/build_orbgun.py`, the Duplet's tool cut down; the source picture, generated 2026-11-07 on a clear ground, not kept in the repository): the grip at (0.1583, 0.6535) of the gun's box, the muzzle at (0.9529, 0.4554). `lying` - a frame of the 2008 cell (32), the gun 24 units long standing on its grip's foot at the bottom, in the middle; `held` - 16 units long in a square of 32, the muzzle 13.75 ahead of the middle and 1.5 over it; `gone` - clear. Sequences alive - `lying`, death - `gone`, as the Duplet's.
- **The shot's look** (`Orbs.Shots`, `TOrbShots`): a `blOrb` bullet is not drawn by its burst (`TBurst.Draw` skips it, and turns a bursting one inactive at once - it has no frames); the game keeps a flock of the moon form's dark orbs (`MoonOrbTint`, the size of a rite's orb) riding the orb bullets where their pictures are (a sprite over the point, as `BulletStretch` has it), ticked once the bullets' hits are settled (`FOrbShots.Tick` after `ResolveHeroBulletHits`); a bullet spent or gone - or one of another look born where a freed one was - leaves its orb to dust; cleared with the bullets at a door. Its sound `orbgun.wav`, `WeaponShotSounds[6]`.
- **The wound** (`TMoonGame.BulletStruckRam`): the honeycomb down, a bullet is met first by the nozzles (`TRam.StrikeNozzles`), then by the hull, then by the wheels; an orb that meets the bare hull while it has lives sinks in - a life (`THullLives.Wound`), `orbhit.wav`, its orb to dust, no sparks; anything else - an orb on a nozzle or a wheel, or on a hull with no life left, and every old bullet - ricochets as since 3.0.110.
- **The nozzles** (`TRam.StrikeNozzles`): each a box of the upright hull under its axle (`NozzleWidth` 14 by `NozzleHeight` 15.8 times how far it is out, from `NozzleHang` 8.9 under the axle), the stretch brought into the upright hull as `StrikeHull` brings it, `TraceEntry` for the face; a hit of the hull for `HullStruckWithin`. Nothing while the nozzles are in. The box does not take the painter's clamp at the road line (`NozzleOut`'s room): on the road, in the ticks the nozzles come out, it reaches a little under the road, where no bullet flies.
- **The hull's lives** (`Game.HullLives`, `THullLives`, the game's): `Arm(share)` - `RamHullLives` 150 times the grade's share (1, 1.5, 2: 150, 225, 300); `Wound`; with none left it takes none (the blast is stage 19). The bar: it comes down from over the top edge over `HullBarDropTicks` 16, then lights `HullBarCells` 30 over `HullBarBootTicks` 30, the terminal's key every `HullBarKeyCells` 3 (`Tick`); a wound flashes it `HullBarHitTicks` 8.
- **The bar** (`Hud.HullBar`, `THudHullBar`, reborn with the HUD): a plate of the panels' colour centred on the screen and on the panels' height, between the monitor (with its wing) and the charge panel; 30 cells of 4 by 6, 2 apart, in the head row's grammar - the thirds' colours, the newest cell of the boot glowing, a red frame and a white cell where a life was just taken.
- **The sounds** (`tools/sounds/orbgun.py`): `orbgun.wav` - a sub thump sliding from 150 to 48 Hz under a breath of dark noise, and a hollow ring of two low partials a fifth apart swelling in behind the blow, 0.42 s, no crack; `orbhit.wav` - the thump an octave lower and longer under a dull clang of three inharmonic low partials, damped hard, 0.6 s.
- **Tests**: `Tests.Game.OrbGun` (new) - `TOrbGunArrivalTests` 9 (nothing till started; it appears after the breath, once; it lies only once put together, once; a start under way changes nothing; taken only while it lies; a clear takes it all; in the middle when he stands aside; the next cell when he stands there, on the wider side; never within a cell of him). `Tests.Game.HullLives` (new) - `THullLivesTests` 7 (nothing till armed; the lives follow the grade; the bar comes down, then lights; the key every few cells; an orb takes a life and flashes the bar; none past the last; a clear). `Tests.Orbs.Shots` (new) - `TOrbShotsTests` 7 (an orb for every orb bullet in flight and none for another; one orb a bullet; the orb rides its bullet's picture; a spent bullet leaves its orb to dust; a bullet gone takes its orb; none armed; a clear). `TRamBrakeTests` +2 (hanging, a bullet from under meets its two nozzles, a nozzle's width each, under the hull, thrown off down; on the road no nozzle to meet). The shroud of the gun, the gun on the road and in the hands, the shot's look, the sounds and the bar: left to the live run.
- First guesses for the live run: the breath of 33 ticks; `ReviveLook` for the gun's shroud; the gun's 24 units on the road and 16 in the hands, its place in the fist, the muzzle's reach 22; the cooldown of 4 and the shot's speed 10, the shot orb's size; the hull's 150 lives; the bar's place, size, drop and boot; both sounds' levels.

## Stages
Each its own commit, each run live.
1. **False ending.** Built (3.0.63), see "Stage 1 as built".
2. **Screen 10 - the double-barrel room.** Built, see "Stage 2 as built".
3. **The double-barrel as a weapon.** Built (3.0.64), see "Stage 3 as built".
4. **The living backdrop.** Built (3.0.65), see "Stage 4 as built". The far ledge comes with its screen (stage 7).
5. **Screen 11.** In steps, each its own commit:
   - 5a. **Closed doors.** Built (3.0.66), see "Stage 5a as built".
   - 5b. **The art of screen 11.** Built, see "Stages 5b and 5c as built".
   - 5c. **Screen 11 as data.** Built, see "Stages 5b and 5c as built".
   - 5d. **The stone wakes.** Built (3.0.67), see "Stage 5d as built".
   - 5e. **The stone is taken.** Built (3.0.69), see "Stage 5e as built".
6. **The energy gauge.** Built (3.0.70), see "Stage 6 as built". The stone line was built with stage 5d.
7. **Screen 12.** Designed (2026-10-23), see "Screen 12"; the art is made. 7a built (3.0.72), see "Stage 7a as built"; 7b built (3.0.73), see "Stage 7b as built"; 7c built, see "Stage 7c as built". In steps: 7a the sphere (`TLifeSphere`, the pickup, its forming and its taking); 7b the convoys and the searchlight (the far ledge as a living backdrop); 7c screen 12 as data (the objects, the far lip, the floor, the events, the shut door).
   **Before stage 8 (decided 2026-10-25): two steps out of Moon2D.dpr.** The boss, the chase, the evolution and the moon form would all land in `TMoonGame`, which lives in the program file, where no test can reach it. So, in order, each in a new chat:
   - 7b closed first: its commits, the live run, the tag 3.0.73.
   - Step 0: done (2026-10-25). `TMoonGame` moved out of `Moon2D.dpr` into `Game/Game.Moon.pas` word for word, the unit joined `Moon2D.dproj`; the program keeps `RunGame`, the discovery of the levels and its uses. One commit, no version; the maps and PORTING-NOTES followed in a commit of their own.
   - Step 1: done (2026-10-25). The orbs and the charge of the form moved out of `TMoonGame` into `Game/Game.HeroOrbs.pas` (`THeroOrbs`): it asks about the hero through probes, strikes through the targets the game hands in (the combat's half - `PopEnemyBullet`, `DangerousMonsterAt`, `WoundByOrb` - stays in `Game.Moon` until the combat is cut out), rewinds itself (`BeginLevel`, `Rewind`, `Arrive`, `Fall`) and does not know the game; 20 tests at its border (`Tests.Game.HeroOrbs`). No version. Stage 8 on lands there: the tank, the trickle and the ceiling of the charge (stage 15) in `FeedEnergy` and `TickAfterCeremony`; the evolution (16) when the charge is full - the first cue the unit tells out, since the jump is the hero's; the moon form (17) is a value of `THero.HeroForm` (`hfMoon` beside `hfNormal` and `hfIce`), which the tint of the aura and the rain and the look of the burst follow, and which the rewind brings back with the charge.
   - Then stage 8. The rest of the split of `TMoonGame` (debug, the gravel trial, combat, level loading) is the debt of version 3.0, paid after the release and before 3.1: PORTING-NOTES, "ДОЛГ ВЕРСИИ 3.0", item д).
   - Alongside, not touching the code: the art of screen 13 - its backdrop drawn on to the right with room to spare for the chase.
8. **Screen 13 - the arena and the hull.** Split (2026-10-25):
   - 8a. **Screen 13 as data and art.** Built (3.0.74), see "Stage 8a as built".
   - 8b. **The machine.** Built (3.0.75), see "Stage 8b as built". Flat floor, closed on both sides; the Ram (169 by 128, facing left for good) on two wheels drives, stops; the contact rule; the mast with its sections; the eye; the TEK shield; the entrance (silence, the drive-in with its rumble, the stop clear of the pads, the mast with the hunter, the boss track on the lock) and its shortened replay after a death. The art is packed, see "Stage 8b, the art"; the sounds are new.
9. **Turret 1, the hunter.** Built (3.0.76), see "Stage 9 as built".
10. **Turret 2, the sweeper.** In steps (decided 2026-10-28):
   - 10a. **The sweeper.** Built (3.0.77), see "Stage 10a as built". The ride to the hero's row, the red barrels, the sweep and its thirds (one sweep, the fork, the ladder), the closed ceiling over a hero on the floor, the turn of a head; the whole barrel pack as the target, about 140 lives a grade, the honeycomb plates under it, its row of lives.
   - 10b. **The bolts of both heads.** Built (3.0.78), see "Stage 10b as built".
   - 10c. **The blast and the wave**, in steps:
     - 10c-1. **The overload and the blast.** Built (3.0.79), see "Stage 10c-1 as built".
     - 10c-2. **The wave.** Built (3.0.80), see "Stage 10c-2 as built".
     - 10c-3. **The pads off.** Built (3.0.81), see "Stage 10c-3 as built".
     - 10c-4. **The retreat before the blast.** Built (3.0.82), see "Stage 10c-4 as built".
     - The wrecks and the blast retuned after the live run (3.0.83), see "The wrecks and the blast retuned".
   - 10d. **The shuttle's capsule.** Built (3.0.84), see "Stage 10d as built".
11. **The belt and the far plane.** Split (2026-10-31):
   - 11a. **The belt.** Built (3.0.86), see "Stage 11a as built". The tiled floor slides with a wrapping offset; the world carried left (the hero, in the air too, and the loot), the hull's wheels turning by the belt, the speed-up and the run-down.
   - 11b. **The backdrop and the fangs.** Split (2026-10-31):
     - 11b-1. **The far plane.** Built (3.0.88), see "Stage 11b-1 as built". The strip of `chase-backdrop.md`, its layers and speeds, the sun.
     - 11b-2. **The fangs.** Built (3.0.89), see "Stage 11b-2 as built". The kit is in `level2-objects.mset` (`s11-fang-needle`, `-crown`, `-double`, `-leaning`); they ride through from right to left at random, behind the play layer, faster than the near hills.
12. **The napalm launcher.** After 14a. The mortar rising on the stern, the shell up and out, the aim a second behind the hero (a point of the road, so it rides the belt: a standing hero gets it on his head, a running one leaves it behind), the warning on the floor, the drop, the splash, the short burn and the soot riding the belt; the leash of "Decided 2026-10-30, the chase", paused while the machine falls back (14b) and folded with phase 3 (14c). No new art: the mortar is in `tek-machine.mset`, the rest is drawn in code ("No new art for the weapons").
13. **The drifting pads and the medkits.** Built (3.0.87), see "Stage 13 as built" and "Stage 13b as built"; the plan below is the first design, the numbers are the built ones. Pads in the world carried by the belt (a pad that leaves the screen on the left is let go), the chunks that lead up to deck 128 every 180 units of belt and the low and middle pads between, medkits on the middle pads of the chunks, gone at the left edge. Numbers: "Decided 2026-10-31, the drifting pads".
14. **Turret 3, the bunker, in three phases** ("Decided 2026-11-01, the bunker in three phases"). Split:
   - 14a. **The bunker and phase 1.** Built (3.0.94), see "Stage 14a as built". It rises and lights its row, the breath before the drive-off counts from the row lit; the shielded turret with the breathing hatch (the column), hit from above at any time ("Decided 2026-11-02, the bunker's top"); the shell on an arc with its ring, bursting on a deck and knocking the pad off, rolling on the road; its lives by thirds; its death (the belt runs on). With 15b.
   - 14b. **Phase 2, the fall back.** Built (3.0.96), see "Stage 14b as built". The eye's warning, the fall back to the middle and the drive back, the throw back over the hull from the road behind the stern, the bunker holding fire (the napalm's pause waits for stage 12); two shells.
   - 14c. **Phase 3, the hunt.** Built (3.0.97), see "Stage 14c as built"; reworked (3.0.98), see "Stage 14c reworked". The change (the alarm, the wave of three dense rows flying in, `Pads.Drift`), the pass through the hero and off the left edge - a hero on the road thrown over the hull to the right - the turn out of sight, the drive in bow first, the hunt with its bow a step behind him, backing only into a hero behind its stern, the ram forward from the road; after the wave the groups again, denser; no napalm in it; the shells in pairs, the second at the pad ahead along his row.
15. **The charge.** Tank, a cut-down trickle, ceilings by heads: 30%, 55%, 95% until the bunker dies. Split (2026-10-30):
   - 15a. **The hunter's and the sweeper's quarters.** Built (3.0.85), see "Stage 15a as built".
   - 15b. **The bunker's half.** Built (3.0.94), see "Stage 14a as built". Its wounds into the tank, the ceiling of 95% while it lives, 100% on its death - then the evolution (stage 16).
16. **The henshin.** Designed (2026-11-04), see "The henshin, the throw and the takeoff". Split, each its own commit and live run:
   - 16a. **The End key's three steps and point A.** Built (3.0.99), see "Stage 16a as built". One setup of the moment before the henshin (the bunker dead, the hunt on, the charge 97), called by the key's third press and by a death after the bunker; respawn with 5 health. First, so every later run starts at the henshin, not five minutes before it.
   - 16b. **The last 3%.** Built (3.0.100), see "Stage 16b as built". The gauge rides from its slot to the top centre and grows; the heartbeat - its sound, the gauge's pulse, the heart monitor in step.
   - 16c. **The brake and the eclipse.** Split (2026-11-04):
     - 16c-1. **The brake and the dark.** Built (3.0.101), see "Stage 16c-1 as built". The Ram brakes and the belt runs down; the hero out of the hull, no wounds; the corona and the diamond ring; the darkness; the boss track paused.
     - 16c-2. **The order reworked, the lift and the beam.** Built (3.0.102), see "Stage 16c-2 as built". The corona, the diamond and the beam out of the disc taken out (decided 2026-11-05, see "100%: the brake and the eclipse"); the control taken at rest, the hero held (`THero`) and lifted a cell, drawn clear of an edge or the hull first; the light going with the gauge; the beam falling from above. The lift waits there for the rite's orbs (16d), which take over its moment.
   - 16d. **The rite.** Built (3.0.103), see "Stage 16d as built"; the hold taken out (3.0.104), see "Stage 16d reworked". Between the catch and the beam: the lift is theirs, the dark deepens with them, the beam comes when the ring is full. The orbs one by one from the surfaces and out of the depth of the far city, the tempo on the beat; the beating ring (the pattern "eclipse"); the collapse; `hfMoon` with the jump +25%.
   - 16e. **The burst.** Built (3.0.106), see "Stage 16e as built". Stronger rays in the moon look, the white flash, the light back at once, the shake; the Ram thrown back and stunned.
17. **The moon form.** Orb gun piercing armour, fire rain, black orb aura. The form's value and its jump came with 16d (3.0.103). The orb gun decided 2026-11-07 (see "The takeoff"), in steps after 18c:
   - 17a. **The orb gun, whole, on a standing world.** Built (3.0.116), see "Stage 17a as built". Re-cut 2026-11-08 (see "Decided 2026-11-08, before the build of 17" and "after the first build of 17a was seen"): the gun appears on the road under the respawn's shroud and is taken as a pickup; `orbgun.mset` from the generated art; the gun in his hands in place of his own; one black orb a shot, a cooldown of 4, endless, its sound; an orb wounds the bare hull, the nozzles and the wheels throw it off; the hull's lives and the long bar along the top of the screen, from the take.
   - 17b. **The chase goes on.** The world moves off again once the gun is his - the road (`FBelt`), the far plane, the fangs, the pads - a smooth start, not a jolt; point B (the hull hanging, the gun in his hands, the bar full, 5 health, the world already moving; the takeoff and the second wheel not played again); the End key's 4th press goes to point B.
18. **Air phase.** Split (2026-11-04), the first three designed in "The henshin, the throw and the takeoff":
   - 18a. **The throw.** Split (2026-11-06):
     - 18a-1. **The grip and the spin.** Built (3.0.107), see "Stage 18a-1 as built". The Ram drives off to the left, a cell of road behind it; the catch, the hero carried to the right over the road, the controls not his till the wheel but for the press; a floor of 4 pads called in; the orbs out of him onto the pads' beams, the jets dead, the pads gliding to a wide arc round the Ram and spinning about their beams on the beat; the hum, the Ram's lights in alarm, the hint.
     - 18a-2. **The volley.** Built (3.0.108), see "Stage 18a-2 as built". The press (or 3 s), each pad let go at its own small delay, the flight off the arc at the honeycomb (the wrecks' flight, aimed), the blasts, a piece of the honeycomb shattered at each hit, the last a big blast, the rest going out cell by cell from the hits outward, the silence.
   - 18b. **The wheel.** Built (3.0.109), see "Stage 18b as built"; the bare hull's ricochet (3.0.110), see "The bare hull's ricochet as built". The front wheel off, the hull's lean and the scrape with sparks, the wheel rolling right and wounding, the control given back.
   - 18c. **The takeoff.** Rewritten 2026-11-07 (see "The takeoff"), split:
     - 18c-1. **Up off the road.** Built (3.0.111), see "Stage 18c-1 as built". The jet out of the front well and the bow lifting; the jet out of the rear well spitting the second wheel at the hero; the hull righting itself in the air, swinging and settling. The hero only watches and jumps the wheel.
     - 18c-2. **It hangs.** Built (3.0.112), see "Stage 18c-2 as built": rocking, over the hero with a lag; the exhausts out; the well's hatch. Point B moved to 17a.
     - 18c-3. **The bay and the nozzles.** Built (3.0.113), see "Stage 18c-3 as built"; the wheels' bounce, the rear one dropped from the risen hull, and the eclipse's charge held to the blast (3.0.114), see "The wheels' bounce and the held charge as built". The generated engine bay (darkened) in both arches and the petal nozzle under its ring, turned with the hull's rock; they slide out at the takeoff before the fire; the fire narrower and shorter. The bar waits for the orb gun (17a). The music is back since the honeycomb (18a-2).
   - 18d and on. **The air fight**, designed in a chat of its own: the takeoff (wheels in, nozzles out), the road's edge and no floor past it, the long pads flying with the hero, the fall back onto a pad; grenades, guns, the hull open to orbs. Likely split into steps when designed. Art: the road's edge, the machine on its nozzles, the long pads with their nozzles to the left. **To design with it (decided 2026-11-07): the nozzles' own swivel.** Since 3.0.113 a nozzle is fixed to the hull and tilts only with its lean and bank; the swivel - turned about the bay's ring by the pace across (flying right, its bottom swings left and the fire pushes it on; braking, the other way), on top of the hull's lean, the fire and the warmth on the road following - was put off to the air fight, where it may become part of the fight (a nozzle turned away as the window to strike). Keep it within about 12 degrees: the actuators are painted fixed in the bay.
19. **The blast.** The explosion, the form off, silence instead of the victory track, the hand-over to the titles (docs/mechanics/finale.md, stage F7; the titles themselves, F1-F6, can be built before the boss).
20. **Tests and docs.** Codebase maps, PORTING-NOTES, TESTING.md, this plan.

## Open
- **The order of 17 and 18c**: the takeoff first (18c-1, 18c-2), the orb gun and the bar after it (17a, 17b) - the hero watches the takeoff with his old gun.
- **The hanging hull within his jump** (noticed 2026-11-08): from the road the hero can jump high enough to touch the hanging hull - its bottom was set just over the moon form's jump (18c), and the touch wounds. To think about, not now.
- **The gallery of 17a**: from the gun to 18d the hull does nothing against him - about 18 s of a held button at the first grade's 150 lives. A stopgap till the air fight is designed.
- **Phase 1 is far too easy** (the live run of 3.0.94). The bunker went from unkillable to very easy at once, and three phases are still ahead of it. Left as it is for now (2026-11-03): the three phases are tuned together once 14c is in - what phase 1 asks of the player (its lives, how often it lobs, how hard the shells and the column punish, how the pads lead up to it), and how the three phases grow from it.
- Phase 3's numbers (3.0.98, live): whether the wave's flight reads as a sight or as noise, and whether it lasts the pass; the turn out of sight and the eye's red at the edge; the bow's step behind him and its speed; whether the left reads as the machine's - a hero behind its stern backed into, one on a pad carried back over its hatch; the ram and the stand after it; the groups after the wave, denser. Then the three phases tuned together (see above).
- The machine after the bunker (3.0.98): it hunts on, invulnerable, till the henshin; at 100 it brakes (16c). It faces right from the hunt on, so the wheel that comes off is the right one and the air phase begins with it facing right.
- The henshin's first guesses (16, 18a-c): the heartbeat's tempo, the gauge's size at the top centre, the rite's count and gaps, the orbs out of the depth, the ring's breath, the burst's strength and flash, the spin-up and the 3 s, the throw's delays, the wheel's speed and height, the lean, the takeoff's height.
- Phase 2's numbers (3.0.96, live): how often it falls back and how long it stands, whether the silence while it falls back leaves too easy a stretch, whether the shells in pairs are too much, whether the way up stays open with them, and how the fling over the hull reads.
- The city (2026-11-01, "Decided 2026-11-01, the city"): its life and the train built (3.0.92, 3.0.93); the train's numbers for the live run (`chase-backdrop.md`, Open).
- The haze of the pad jets on screen 13: back once the far plane is drawn into a texture the haze can bend.
- The fangs (11b-2, live): their speed (20 - a fang crosses the screen in about 30 s, a calm middle ground rather than posts flashing by), how many, how high the tall ones, how dark.
- The entrance's numbers (8b, live): the drive-in's speed, the breath before the mast, how fast the mast rises; whether the shortened replay after a death reads right.
- The press and the hunt of act 1 (stage 9): the numbers after the live run - the low line, how fast it presses, the legs of the hunt.
- The hunter's lives (100 a grade) and its bursts by the thirds: tuned live.
- The mast against the pads (8b, live): the sweeper at full mast must reach the middle pad's row; the bunker stands low so its hatch is under the middle pad and the high ones.
- The sweeper cannot reach the floor: the floor is the ram's, and the closed ceiling keeps the hero from jumping out of it. Check live that a hero on the floor is driven at often enough and that the gaps between sweeps leave a way up.
- The bunker's lid: shut, it is the open lid turned and moved over the hatch (the generation has no shut bunker); if it reads poorly, one more generation of the bunker with the hatch shut.
- The city road against the dark wall may read too bright: a tint of about 80% on its 22 tiles, after the live run.
- The traffic and the searchlight of 13: kept as a living backdrop through the fight, or cut back - after the live run.
- The crust of screen 12 is below 6 px a unit (1527 px for 328 units): regenerate in two halves for 4K.
- The 4K room of screen 10: four objects of 256 units from an upscale.
- The eclipse on taking the stone: kept in reserve.
- The Duplet after level 2: does the hero keep it on the next levels, where small monsters come close?
- Turret patterns and the numbers of the charge (K, drain, trickle) - first guesses, tuned live.
- What the black orb aura and the fire rain do in numbers against the ice form's.
- Three turrets or four. A fourth only if the chase is too short: the machine drops a plate and turret 4 sits low on the rear hitch, hit from the floor - turret 3 from above, turret 4 from below.
- The low pads by the walls (`s13-pad-1`, `s13-pad-4`): a quiet harbour from the sweeper or not - it cannot reach under its roof, and the ram does not press a hero on a low pad. Left for later (2026-10-28); check live.
- The fight in motion (2026-11-01), to settle before stage 18d (the henshin and the throw play on a stopped world, decided 2026-11-04): the belt after the takeoff - the same speed as the chase or faster; what presses the hero in the air in place of the napalm's leash; how the long pads move among themselves; how the bottom of the screen reads as an abyss once the road is gone (the near hills reach the bottom edge and must not read as a floor to drop onto); the end - whether the blast stops the world, the pads and the far plane coming to rest with the city in sight, as the silence falls.
- The bunker in three phases (2026-11-01): the shells' height, flight and count by phase; how often phase 2 falls back and how long it stays; the shape of phase 3's rows (density, gaps, how far ahead the shells knock); whether knocking pads off in phase 1 is too much - checked live.

## Risks
- *Gone in 3.0.113: a shed wheel's well is hollow till its bay comes out, and the bays fill the arches (`WellOpen`).* The wheels shed, still in the body (3.0.111): the body's grid was read with both wheels' circles solid.
- The nozzles on the road (3.0.113): sliding out with the hull still on the road, a nozzle stops at the road line and comes out the rest of the way as the hull lifts; the rear one comes out behind its wheel, its corners a unit under the wheel's rim. Check live that the half-out front nozzle reads as pressed to the road, not cut, and that the rear one does not show under its wheel.
- *Gone in 3.0.116: the nozzles throw a shot off themselves (`TRam.StrikeNozzles`).* The nozzles and bullets (3.0.113): the nozzles hang about 5 units under the hull's bottom and are not in the body's grid - a shot from under the hanging hull passed through them to the bay over them.
- The pose's touch (3.0.111): the hero's box brought back into the upright hull is the bounding box of its turned corners - wider by a little at 10 degrees; check live that a hero beside the lifted bow is not touched by air.
- The throw with too few pads: the world stops wherever the drift has left them - after the wave or a run of knocked-off pads there may be one or none on the screen. 3.0.107: a floor of 4, the missing ones called in from the pool from beyond the edges as he is carried, and at most 7 taken; check live whether 4 and 7 read right.
- The hull's lean (18b): the hull is drawn in layers (`Monsters.Hull`), each to be turned about the rear wheel's contact, the well, the mast stump and the eye with them - check the layers turn as one. 3.0.109: every layer, the eye, the lamps and the smoke points turn through `THullStand`; the mast at rest is not drawn while it leans (no stump shows at rest); the plough digs about 7 under the road line - check live that it reads as the nose dug in, not the hull sunk through the road.
- The darkness and the far plane: one overlay over everything below the beam must not catch the HUD, the beam or the orbs; the far city's orbs grow out of the depth under it and must still read in the dark.
- The hull passable through the scene (3.0.104): the hero runs through the halted Ram; with the dark over it and nothing to wound, it should read as a scene, not a bug - if it reads as a bug, the hull's box shoves him out sideways, no roof to stand on.
- *Gone in 3.0.107: the hero is carried to the right over the road, not lifted a cell where he is.* The lift under a low pad (3.0.102, now 18a's): the lowest pads of the chase stand at 288, so a hero lifted a cell off the road (his head at 288) meets a pad hanging right over him and is drawn over its underside. Rare once the road stands; left to the live run - if it reads wrong, a deck over his head becomes an obstacle for `Game.Hoist` as the hull is.
- The shout over the gauge (3.0.103): **EVOLUTION** comes at the catch on the level title's line, where the gauge still burns till the first orb sits. If the two read as a mess, the shout waits for the lift or goes lower.
- The fangs as matter (3.0.103): a fang gives its orbs off a column down its middle; a leaning one or a crown whose tip is off its middle sends them out of the air beside it. If it reads wrong, the fangs leave the rite's matter.
- The specks out of the city (3.0.103): drawn over the dark, as every orb is, so they read; small and dim at first, they may read as sparks rather than as orbs out of the depth.
- Under the dark (3.0.101): the hero's own bullets fired before the control is taken are drawn over it, as everything after the hero is; if they read wrong, they go under it.
- The giant double-barrel's spread meets the honeycomb before the turrets: a lot of flares and no damage until the player understands the turrets are the target. Probably fine - arena 2 taught the honeycomb.
- The charge must never stall: the trickle plus the ceiling guarantee 100% at the third turret's death; check live that the gauge does not read as "it fills by itself, the turrets do not matter".
- The air phase with grenades and guns after a long fight - one death replays the whole fight. Keep the fight short.
- The floor that ends mid-fight: the collision grid of screen 13 has stood flat wall to wall; the road's edge takes it away under the hero on the move.
- The belt and the plain jump: the hatch height must be reachable from the pads with the plain jump - the +25% comes only with the evolution.
- The pads and the belt: a pad that leaves the screen on the left must be allowed to (the pad code may assume pads stay on the screen); and a hero on a pad that the belt carries and a shot or a knock-off drops must never be left hanging in the air.
- Phase 2's corridor: between the leash (171) and a ram at the middle - the bunker and the napalm hold their fire while it falls back; check live that the room is enough.
- The fling over the hull (3.0.96): in its first ticks he is still under the roof line and passes the stern's upper corner - out of the machine's way, but it may read as flying through it; flung while the machine still lunges, he crosses less of it and may come down inside the hull (the way out of it covers that). Where he comes down - about 330 units left - may be the napalm's leash once stage 12 lands.
- Phase 3's pass: the three rows must be on the screen before the machine starts back through the hero, and the throw over a 128 tall hull at the left edge must clear it. 3.0.97: the pass waits for the rows' cue, and the throw has a way of its own of 3 a tick against the road's 1; check live that he clears the hull without a hand on the keys. 3.0.98: the pass goes on off the screen and the throw is to the right at any touch on the road, the hull driving away under him.
- The rows and the pool (3.0.97): the first stretch and the rows on the screen take up to about 50 pads of the 52 - a pool run dry leaves holes, which read as the shells' work. 3.0.98: the wave alone takes about 40, and the groups after it draw on what is left while it rides off; a group short of pads is a road with holes. The middle row (192) stands just over the bunker's top (about 204): check live that the dome reads under it, not through it.
